  //     };

  //     window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);

  //     console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
  //     console.log("TTS TEST:", pageText);


  //   } catch (error) {
  //     console.error("TTS failed: ", error);
  //     alert("Unable to read this page aloud.");
  //   }
  // }

  // function handleStopSpeech() {
  //   window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  // }


  async function handleSpeakPage() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      alert("Text-to-Speech is not supported in this browser.");
      return;
    }

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    // Resume from paused position.
    if (synth.paused) {
      synth.resume();
      setTtsStatus("playing");
      return;
    }

    // Avoid restarting speech when already playing.
    if (synth.speaking || ttsStatus === "loading") {
      return;
    }

    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

    try {
      setTtsStatus("loading");

      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;
      const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
      const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

      console.table(
        textContent.items.slice(0,30).map((item, index)=>({
          index,
          text: item.str,
          fontName: item.fontName,
          hasEOL: item.hasEOL,
        }))
      )

      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      const pageText = textContent.items
        .map((item) => item.str || "")
        .join(" ")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();

      if (!pageText) {
        setTtsStatus("idle");
        alert("No readable text found on this page.");
        return;
      }

      console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("TTS TEXT LENGTH: ", pageText.length);
      console.log("TTS TEXT:", pageText);

      // Divide text into smaller pieces.
      const maxChunkLength = 200;
      const chunks = [];
      let remainingText = pageText;

      while (remainingText.length > 0) {
        if (remainingText.length <= maxChunkLength) {
          chunks.push(remainingText);
          break;
        }

        let splitAt = remainingText.lastIndexOf(
          " ",
          maxChunkLength
        );

        if (splitAt <= 0) {
          splitAt = maxChunkLength;
        }

        const chunk = remainingText.slice(0, splitAt).trim();

        if (chunk) {
          chunks.push(chunk);
        }

        remainingText = remainingText.slice(splitAt).trim();
      }

      console.log("TOTAL TTS CHUNKS:", chunks.length);

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );

      let chunkIndex = 0;

      const speakNextChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
          return;
        }

        if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
          setTtsStatus("idle");
          console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED");
          return;
        }


        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          chunks[chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) {
          utterance.voice = voice;
        }

        utterance.onstart = () => {
          if (sessionId === speechSessionRef.current) {
            setTtsStatus("playing");
          }
        };

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          chunkIndex += 1;

          speakNextChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        console.log(
          `READING CHUNK ${chunkIndex + 1}/${chunks.length}`
        );

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      speakNextChunk();
    } catch (error) {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.error("TTS failed:", error);
      setTtsStatus("idle");
    }
  }

  function handlePauseSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    window.speechSynthesis.pause();
    setTtsStatus("paused");
  }

  function handleStopSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    // Invalidate pending and future chunks.
    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("idle");
  }



  async function handleTranslate() {
    if (!extractedText.trim()) {
      alert("Please extract the PDF text first.");
      return;
    }

    if (selectedLanguage === "en") {
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setTranslating(true);
      setTranslationError("");

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(extractedText);

      // const testText =
      //   "At the core of quantum information processing is the quantum bit, or qubit.";

      // const testText = "The sun rises in the east.";

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(testText);

      setTranslationProgress("Preparing translation...");

      const translated = await translateToHindi(
        extractedText,
        (completed, total) => {
          setTranslationProgress(
            `Translating ${completed} of ${total} chunks...`
          );
        }
      );

      setTranslatedText(translated);

      console.log("HINDI TRANSLATION:", translated);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Translation failed:", error);

      setTranslationError(
        error?.message || "Unable to translate this page."
      );

    } finally {
      setTranslating(false);
      setTranslationProgress("");
    }
  }



  if (!product) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <h1>Book not found</h1>
        <Link to="/">Return to SKS Dream</Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="reader-page">
      <header className="reader-header">
        <Link to="/" className="reader-back">
          ← Back to SKS Dream
        </Link>
        <span className="reader-brand">SKS Dream</span>
      </header>

      <section className="reader-heading">
        <p className="reader-eyebrow">DIGITAL LIBRARY</p>
        <h1>{product.name}</h1>
        <p>Settle in, turn the pages, and enjoy your reading experience.</p>
      </section>

      {loading && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="status">
          Preparing your book…
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="alert">
          <p>{error}</p>
          <Link to="/">Return to products</Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* {!loading && !error && pages.length > 0 && ( */}
      {!loading && !error && pdfDocument && totalPages > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* Zooom Effects */}

          <nav
            className="reader-zoom-toolbar"
            aria-label="Reading zoom controls"
          >
            <span className="reader-zoom-label">Reading View</span>

            <div className="reader-zoom-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomOut}
                disabled={zoom <= 75}
                aria-label="Zoom out"
              >
                -
              </button>

              <span className="reader-zoom-level">{zoom}%</span>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomIn}
                disabled={zoom >= 200}
                aria-label="Zoom in"
              >
                +
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="reader-zoom-reset"
                onClick={resetZoom}
              >
                Reset
              </button>
            </div>
          </nav>

          <div className="reader-book-viewport">
            <div className="reader-book-zoom" style={{ zoom: zoom / 100 }}>
              <section className="book-stage" aria-label="Interactive book">
                <HTMLFlipBook
                  ref={flipBookRef}
                  width={380}
                  height={520}
                  size="stretch"
                  minWidth={260}
                  maxWidth={760}
                  minHeight={360}
                  maxHeight={620}
                  showCover
                  usePortrait
                  mobileScrollSupport
                  drawShadow
                  flippingTime={650}
                  maxShadowOpacity={0.45}
                  onFlip={(event) => {
                    // window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                    handleStopSpeech();

                    setCurrentPage(event.data);
                    setExtractedText("");
                    setTranslatedText("");
                    setTranslationError("");
                  }}
                  className="book-flip"
                >
                  {/* {pages.map((page, index) => (
                    <FlipPage key={index} page={page} number={index + 1} />
                  ))} */}

                  {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => (
                    <FlipPage
                      key={`${product.id}-${index}`}
                      pdfDocument={pdfDocument}
                      number={index + 1}
                      shouldRender={Math.abs(index - currentPage) <= 3}
                    />
                  ))}

                </HTMLFlipBook>
              </section>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "20px 0" }} >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={extractCurrentPageText}
              disabled={extracting}
            >
              {extracting ? "Extracting..." : "Extract Page Text"}
            </button>

            <div style={{ marginTop: "15px" }}>

              <label htmlFor="pdf-language">
                Select Language:{" "}
              </label>

              <select id="pdf-language"
                value={selectedLanguage}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setSelectedLanguage(event.target.value)
                  setTranslatedText("");
                  setTranslationError("");
                }}
              >
                <option value="en">English</option>
                <option value="hi">Hindi</option>

              </select>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTranslate}
                disabled={translating || extracting || !extractedText}
                style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
              >
                {translating ? "Translating..." : "Translate"}
              </button>

              {translating && translationProgress && (
                <p
                  style={{
                    marginTop: "10px",
                    fontSize: "14px",
                    color: "#555",
                  }}
                >
                  {translationProgress}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {extractedText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "300px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Extracted PDF Text</h3>

                {/* <p>{extractedText}</p> */}
                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >{
                    selectedLanguage === "hi" && translatedText
                      ? translatedText
                      : extractedText
                  }</p>

                {translationError && (
                  <p
                    style={{ color: "red", marginTop: "10px" }}
                  >{translationError}</p>
                )}

              </div>
            )}

          </div>

          <div className="reader-controls">

            <select
              value={ttsLanguage}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setTtsLanguage(event.target.value);
                setSelectedVoice("");
              }}
            >
              <option value="en-IN">English</option>
              <option value="hi-IN">Hindi</option>
              <option value="mr-IN">Marathi</option>
              <option value="sa-IN">Sanskrit</option>
            </select>

            <select
              value={selectedVoice}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setSelectedVoice(event.target.value);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">Default Voice</option>

              {ttsVoices
                .filter((voice) =>
                  voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
                    ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
                  )
                )
                .map((voice) => (
                  <option
                    key={`${voice.name}-${voice.lang}`}
                    value={voice.name}
                  >
                    {voice.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={ttsSpeed}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setTtsSpeed(Number(event.target.value))
              }
            >
              <option value={0.75}>0.75× Slow</option>
              <option value={1}>1× Normal</option>
              <option value={1.25}>1.25× Fast</option>
              <option value={1.5}>1.5× Faster</option>
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSpeakPage}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "loading"}
            >
              {ttsStatus === "paused"
                ? "▶ Resume"
                : ttsStatus === "playing"
                  ? "🔊 Playing"
                  : "🔊 Listen"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handlePauseSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus !== "playing"}
            >
              ⏸ Pause
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStopSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "idle"}
            >
              ⏹ Stop
            </button>

          </div>

          <nav className="reader-controls" aria-label="Book navigation">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goPrevious}
              disabled={currentPage === 0}
            >
              ← Previous
            </button>

            <span>
              {/* Page {currentPage + 1} of {pages.length} */}
              Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goNext}
              // disabled={currentPage >= pages.length - 1}
              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
            >
              Next →
            </button>
          </nav>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

const FlipPage = forwardRef(function FlipPage(
  { pdfDocument, number, shouldRender },
  ref
) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pdfDocument || !shouldRender) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let renderTask;

    async function renderPage() {
      try {
        const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(number);

        if (cancelled) return;

        const canvas = canvasRef.current;
        if (!canvas) return;

        const viewport = pdfPage.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

        canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
        canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

        renderTask = pdfPage.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        });

        await renderTask.promise;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error?.name !== "RenderingCancelledException") {
          console.error("Page rendering failed:", error);
        }
      }
    }

    renderPage();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      renderTask?.cancel();
    };
  }, [pdfDocument, number, shouldRender]);

  return (
    <div className="flip-page" ref={ref}>
      <div className="flip-page-content">
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
      </div>
      <span className="flip-page-number">{number}</span>
    </div>
  );
});

  const speechSessionRef = useRef(0);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(100);
  const [pdfDocument, setPdfDocument] = useState(null);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0);
  const [extractedText, setExtractedText] = useState("");
  const [extracting, setExtracting] = useState(false);

  const [docxText, setDocxText] = useState("");
  const [docxLoading, setDocxLoading] = useState(false);
  const [docxError, setDocxError] = useState("");

  const [selectedLanguage, setSelectedLanguage] = useState("en");
  const [translatedText, setTranslatedText] = useState("");
  const [translating, setTranslating] = useState(false);
  const [translationError, setTranslationError] = useState("");

  const [translationProgress, setTranslationProgress] = useState("");


  const [ttsLanguage, setTtsLanguage] = useState("en-IN");
  const [ttsSpeed, setTtsSpeed] = useState(1);
  const [ttsVoices, setTtsVoices] = useState([]);
  const [selectedVoice, setSelectedVoice] = useState("");
  const [ttsStatus, setTtsStatus] = useState("idle");

  const [product, setProduct] = useState(null);
  const [productLoading, setProductLoading] = useState(true);



  function zoomIn() {
    setZoom((current) => Math.min(current + 25, 200));
  }

  function zoomOut() {
    setZoom((current) => Math.max(current - 25, 75));
  }

  function resetZoom() {
    setZoom(100);
  }

  // const product = products.find((item) => item.id === id);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadProduct() {
      setProductLoading(true);

      try {
        const book = await getBookById(id);

        if (!cancelled) {
          setProduct(book);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Book loading failed:", error);

        if (!cancelled) {
          setProduct(null);
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setProductLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadProduct();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [id]);


  useEffect(() => {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    const loadVoices = () => {
      const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();

      setTtsVoices(voices);

      console.log(
        "AVAILABLE TTS VOICES:",
        voices.map((voice) => ({
          name: voice.name,
          lang: voice.lang,
        }))
      );
    };

    loadVoices();

    window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener(
      "voiceschanged",
      loadVoices
    );

    return () => {
      window.speechSynthesis.removeEventListener(
        "voiceschanged",
        loadVoices
      );
    };
  }, []);

      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      setPages([]);
      setCurrentPage(0);
      setPdfDocument(null);
      setTotalPages(0);

      if (!product?.pdfUrl) {
        setError("This book does not have a PDF available yet.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const pdfUrl = new URL(product.pdfUrl, window.location.origin).href;
        loadingTask = pdfjsLib.getDocument({ url: pdfUrl });
        const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;

        if (cancelled) return;

        setPdfDocument(pdf);
        setTotalPages(pdf.numPages);

        // const renderedPages = [];

        // for (let pageNumber = 1; pageNumber <= pdf.numPages; pageNumber++) {

      } catch (err) {
        if (cancelled || err?.name === "AbortException") {
          return;
        }

        console.error("PDF loading failed:", err);

        setError(
          "We couldn't open this PDF. Check the file path and try again."
        );

      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }




    loadPdf();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      loadingTask?.destroy();
    };
  }, [product?.pdfUrl]);

  async function handleReadDocx() {

    if (!product?.docxUrl) return;


    try {
      setDocxLoading(true);
      setDocxError("")

      const text = await extractDocxText(product.docxUrl);
      setDocxText(text);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("DOCX extraction failed:", error);
      setDocxError(error.message || "Unable to read DOCX.");
    } finally {
      setDocxLoading(false);
    }

  }

  async function handleTestOCR() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

      console.log("STARTING OCR TEST:", pageNumber);

      console.log("FINAL OCR RESULT:", text);
    } catch (error) {

    }
  }

  console.log("BOOK PRODUCT:", product);

      );

      setExtractedText(result.text);
    } catch (err) {

    }
  }

  async function extractCurrentPageText() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    try {
      setExtracting(true);
      setExtractedText("");
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");

      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const cleanText = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl: product?.docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });

      setExtractedText(cleanText);

      console.log("CLEAN PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("CLEAN TEXT:", cleanText);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Clean text extraction failed:", error);
      alert(error.message || "Unable to extract page text.");
    } finally {
      setExtracting(false);
    }
  }

  // async function handleSpeakPage() {
  //   if (!pdfDocument) {
  //     alert("PDF is not ready yet.");
  //     return;
  //   }

  //   if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
  //     alert("Text-to-Speech is not supported in this browser.");
  //     return;
  //   }


  //   try {
  //     // stop any previous speech
  //     window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

  //     // Get the current PDF page.
  //     const pageNumber = currentPage + 1
  //     const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);

  //     // Extract text directly from PDF.js
  //     const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

  //     const pageText = textContent.items
  //       .map((item) => item.str || "")
  //       .join(" ")
  //       .trim();

  //     if (!pageText) {
  //       alert("No readable text found on this page.");
  //       return;
  //     }

  //     // Create speech.

  //     const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(pageText);

  //     utterance.lang = "en-IN";
  //     utterance.rate = 1;
  //     utterance.pitch = 1;
  //     utterance.volume = 1;

  //     utterance.onerror = (event) => {
  //       if (event.error !== "canceled") {
  //         console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
  //       }
  //     };

  //     window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);

  //     console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
  //     console.log("TTS TEST:", pageText);


  //   } catch (error) {
  //     console.error("TTS failed: ", error);
  //     alert("Unable to read this page aloud.");
  //   }
  // }

  // function handleStopSpeech() {
  //   window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  // }


  async function handleSpeakPage() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      alert("Text-to-Speech is not supported in this browser.");
      return;
    }

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    // Resume from paused position.
    if (synth.paused) {
      synth.resume();
      setTtsStatus("playing");
      return;
    }

    // Avoid restarting speech when already playing.
    if (synth.speaking || ttsStatus === "loading") {
      return;
    }

    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

    try {
      setTtsStatus("loading");

      // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;
      // const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
      // const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

      // console.table(
      //   textContent.items.slice(0, 30).map((item, index) => ({
      //     index,
      //     text: item.str,
      //     fontName: item.fontName,
      //     hasEOL: item.hasEOL,
      //   }))
      // )

      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });


      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      // const pageText = textContent.items
      //   .map((item) => item.str || "")
      //   .join(" ")
      //   .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      //   .trim();


      if (!pageText) {
        setTtsStatus("idle");
        alert("No readable text found on this page.");
        return;
      }

      console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("TTS TEXT LENGTH: ", pageText.length);
      console.log("TTS TEXT:", pageText);

      // Divide text into smaller pieces.
      const maxChunkLength = 200;
      const chunks = [];
      let remainingText = pageText;

      while (remainingText.length > 0) {
        if (remainingText.length <= maxChunkLength) {
          chunks.push(remainingText);
          break;
        }

        let splitAt = remainingText.lastIndexOf(
          " ",
          maxChunkLength
        );

        if (splitAt <= 0) {
          splitAt = maxChunkLength;
        }

        const chunk = remainingText.slice(0, splitAt).trim();

        if (chunk) {
          chunks.push(chunk);
        }

        remainingText = remainingText.slice(splitAt).trim();
      }

      console.log("TOTAL TTS CHUNKS:", chunks.length);

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );


      let chunkIndex = 0;

      const speakNextChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
          return;
        }

        if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
          setTtsStatus("idle");
          console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED");
          return;
        }


        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          chunks[chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) {
          utterance.voice = voice;
        }

        utterance.onstart = () => {
          if (sessionId === speechSessionRef.current) {
            setTtsStatus("playing");
          }
        };

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          chunkIndex += 1;

          speakNextChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        console.log(
          `READING CHUNK ${chunkIndex + 1}/${chunks.length}`
        );

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      speakNextChunk();
    } catch (error) {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.error("TTS failed:", error);
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      alert(error.message || "Unable to rea this page aloud.")
    }
  }

  function handlePauseSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    window.speechSynthesis.pause();
    setTtsStatus("paused");
  }

  function handleStopSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    // Invalidate pending and future chunks.
    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("idle");
  }



  async function handleTranslate() {
    if (!extractedText.trim()) {
      alert("Please extract the PDF text first.");
      return;
    }

    if (selectedLanguage === "en") {
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setTranslating(true);
      setTranslationError("");

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(extractedText);

      // const testText =
      //   "At the core of quantum information processing is the quantum bit, or qubit.";

      // const testText = "The sun rises in the east.";

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(testText);

      setTranslationProgress("Preparing translation...");

      const translated = await translateToHindi(
        extractedText,
        (completed, total) => {
          setTranslationProgress(
            `Translating ${completed} of ${total} chunks...`
          );
        }
      );

      setTranslatedText(translated);

      console.log("HINDI TRANSLATION:", translated);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Translation failed:", error);

      setTranslationError(
        error?.message || "Unable to translate this page."
      );

    } finally {
      setTranslating(false);
      setTranslationProgress("");
    }
  }


  if (productLoading) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <p>Loading your book...</p>
      </main>
    )
  }


  if (!product) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <h1>Book not found</h1>
        <Link to="/">Return to SKS Dream</Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="reader-page">
      <header className="reader-header">
        <Link to="/" className="reader-back">
          ← Back to SKS Dream
        </Link>
        <span className="reader-brand">SKS Dream</span>
      </header>

      <section className="reader-heading">
        <p className="reader-eyebrow">DIGITAL LIBRARY</p>
        <h1>{product.name}</h1>
        <p>Settle in, turn the pages, and enjoy your reading experience.</p>
      </section>

      {loading && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="status">
          Preparing your book…
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="alert">
          <p>{error}</p>
          <Link to="/">Return to products</Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* {!loading && !error && pages.length > 0 && ( */}
      {!loading && !error && pdfDocument && totalPages > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* Zooom Effects */}

          <nav
            className="reader-zoom-toolbar"
            aria-label="Reading zoom controls"
          >
            <span className="reader-zoom-label">Reading View</span>

            <div className="reader-zoom-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomOut}
                disabled={zoom <= 75}
                aria-label="Zoom out"
              >
                -
              </button>

              <span className="reader-zoom-level">{zoom}%</span>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomIn}
                disabled={zoom >= 200}
                aria-label="Zoom in"
              >
                +
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="reader-zoom-reset"
                onClick={resetZoom}
              >
                Reset
              </button>
            </div>
          </nav>

          <div className="reader-book-viewport">
            <div className="reader-book-zoom" style={{ zoom: zoom / 100 }}>
              <section className="book-stage" aria-label="Interactive book">
                <HTMLFlipBook
                  ref={flipBookRef}
                  width={380}
                  height={520}
                  size="stretch"
                  minWidth={260}
                  maxWidth={760}
                  minHeight={360}
                  maxHeight={620}
                  showCover
                  usePortrait
                  mobileScrollSupport
                  drawShadow
                  flippingTime={650}
                  maxShadowOpacity={0.45}
                  onFlip={(event) => {
                    // window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                    handleStopSpeech();

                    setCurrentPage(event.data);
                    setExtractedText("");
                    setTranslatedText("");
                    setTranslationError("");
                  }}
                  className="book-flip"
                >
                  {/* {pages.map((page, index) => (
                    <FlipPage key={index} page={page} number={index + 1} />
                  ))} */}

                  {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => (
                    <FlipPage
                      key={`${product.id}-${index}`}
                      pdfDocument={pdfDocument}
                      number={index + 1}
                      shouldRender={Math.abs(index - currentPage) <= 3}
                    />
                  ))}

                </HTMLFlipBook>
              </section>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "20px 0" }} >

            {product?.docxUrl && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleReadDocx}
                disabled={docxLoading}
                style={{ marginRight: "10px" }}
              >
                {docxLoading ? "Loading DOCX..." : "Read Clean DOCX Text"}
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={extractCurrentPageText}
              disabled={extracting}
            >
              {extracting ? "Extracting..." : "Extract Page Text"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestOCR}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Marathi OCR
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestDocxMatch}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              TEST DOCX Matching
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestPageBoundaries}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Page Boundaries
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestCleanPage}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Clean Page
            </button>

            <div style={{ marginTop: "15px" }}>

              <label htmlFor="pdf-language">
                Select Language:{" "}
              </label>

              <select id="pdf-language"
                value={selectedLanguage}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setSelectedLanguage(event.target.value)
                  setTranslatedText("");
                  setTranslationError("");
                }}
              >
                <option value="en">English</option>
                <option value="hi">Hindi</option>

              </select>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTranslate}
                disabled={translating || extracting || !extractedText}
                style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
              >
                {translating ? "Translating..." : "Translate"}
              </button>

              {translating && translationProgress && (
                <p
                  style={{
                    marginTop: "10px",
                    fontSize: "14px",
                    color: "#555",
                  }}
                >
                  {translationProgress}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {extractedText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "300px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Extracted PDF Text</h3>

                {/* <p>{extractedText}</p> */}
                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >{
                    selectedLanguage === "hi" && translatedText
                      ? translatedText
                      : extractedText
                  }</p>

                {translationError && (
                  <p
                    style={{ color: "red", marginTop: "10px" }}
                  >{translationError}</p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* DOCX extraction error */}
            {docxError && (
              <p role="alert" style={{ color: "red" }}>
                {docxError}
              </p>
            )}

            {/* Clean DOCX text display */}
            {docxText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "350px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Clean DOCX Text</h3>

                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >
                  {docxText}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}


          </div>

          <div className="reader-controls">

            <select
              value={ttsLanguage}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setTtsLanguage(event.target.value);
                setSelectedVoice("");
              }}
            >
              <option value="en-IN">English</option>
              <option value="hi-IN">Hindi</option>
              <option value="mr-IN">Marathi</option>
              <option value="sa-IN">Sanskrit</option>
            </select>

            <select
              value={selectedVoice}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setSelectedVoice(event.target.value);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">Default Voice</option>

              {ttsVoices
                .filter((voice) =>
                  voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
                    ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
                  )
                )
                .map((voice) => (
                  <option
                    key={`${voice.name}-${voice.lang}`}
                    value={voice.name}
                  >
                    {voice.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={ttsSpeed}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setTtsSpeed(Number(event.target.value))
              }
            >
              <option value={0.75}>0.75× Slow</option>
              <option value={1}>1× Normal</option>
              <option value={1.25}>1.25× Fast</option>
              <option value={1.5}>1.5× Faster</option>
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSpeakPage}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "loading"}
            >
              {ttsStatus === "paused"
                ? "▶ Resume"
                : ttsStatus === "playing"
                  ? "🔊 Playing"
                  : "🔊 Listen"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handlePauseSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus !== "playing"}
            >
              ⏸ Pause
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStopSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "idle"}
            >
              ⏹ Stop
            </button>

          </div>

          <nav className="reader-controls" aria-label="Book navigation">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goPrevious}
              disabled={currentPage === 0}
            >
              ← Previous
            </button>

            <span>
              {/* Page {currentPage + 1} of {pages.length} */}
              Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goNext}
              // disabled={currentPage >= pages.length - 1}
              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
            >
              Next →
            </button>
          </nav>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}





BookTextService.js

import mammoth from "mammoth";
import { createWorker } from "tesseract.js";

const docxTextCache = new Map();

const ocrPageCache = new WeakMap();
let ocrWorkerPromise = null;

async function getOCRWorker() {
  if (!ocrWorkerPromise) {
    ocrWorkerPromise = createWorker(["mar", "hin"]).catch((err) => {
      ocrWorkerPromise = null;
      throw err;
    });
  }

  return ocrWorkerPromise;
}

export async function extractDocxText(docxUrl) {
  if (!docxTextCache.has(docxUrl)) {
    const request = (async () => {
      const response = await fetch(docxUrl);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`DOCX loading failed: ${response.status}`);
      }

      const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
      const result = await mammoth.extractRawText({ arrayBuffer });

      return result.value.trim();
    })();

    docxTextCache.set(docxUrl, request);
  }

  try {
    return await docxTextCache.get(docxUrl);
  } catch (error) {
    docxTextCache.delete(docxUrl);
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function getBookText({ docxUrl, pdfDocument, pageNumber }) {
  if (docxUrl) {
    const text = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);

    return {
      source: "docx",
      text,
      pageAligned: false,
    };
  }

  if (!pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("PDF document and page number are required");
  }

  const page = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
  const content = await page.getTextContent();

  return {
    source: "pdf",
    text: content.items
      .map((item) => item.str || "")
      .join(" ")
      .trim(),
    pageAligned: true,
  };
}

export async function getReadablePageText({
  docxUrl,
  pdfDocument,
  pageNumber,
}) {
  if (!pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("PDF document and page number are required.");
  }

  if (!docxUrl) {
    throw new Error(
      "Clean DOCX text is unavailable for this book."
    );
  }

  const result = await extractVerifiedDocxPage({
    docxUrl,
    pdfDocument,
    pageNumber,
  });
  

  if (!result.text?.trim()) {
    throw new Error("No readable text found on this page.");
  }

  return result.text;
}

export async function testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber) {
  if (
    !pdfDocument ||
    !Number.isInteger(pageNumber) ||
    pageNumber < 1 ||
    pageNumber > pdfDocument.numPages
  ) {
    throw new Error("Valid PDF document and page number are required.");
  }

  let pageCache = ocrPageCache.get(pdfDocument);

  if (!pageCache) {
    pageCache = new Map();
    ocrPageCache.set(pdfDocument, pageCache);
  }

  if (!pageCache.has(pageNumber)) {
    const request = (async () => {
      const page = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
      const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 2 });

      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

      canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
      canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

      try {
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

        if (!context) {
          throw new Error("Canvas context is unavailable.");
        }

        await page.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        }).promise;

        const worker = await getOCRWorker();
        const result = await worker.recognize(canvas);

        return {
          text: result.data.text.trim(),
          confidence: result.data.confidence,
        };
      } finally {
        canvas.width = 0;
        canvas.height = 0;
      }
    })();

    pageCache.set(pageNumber, request);
  }

  try {
    const result = await pageCache.get(pageNumber);

    console.log("OCR PAGE:", pageNumber);
    console.log("OCR CONFIDENCE:", result.confidence);
    console.log("OCR TEXT:", result.text);

    return result.text;
  } catch (error) {
    pageCache.delete(pageNumber);
    throw error;
  }
}

function normalizeForMatching(text) {
  return text
    .normalize("NFC")
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, "")
    .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]/gu, "")
    .toLowerCase();
}

function getTextParagraphs(text) {
  return text
    .split(/\n+/)
    .map((paragraph) => paragraph.trim())
    .filter((paragraph) => paragraph.length > 20);
}

export async function testDocxPageMatch({ docxUrl, pdfDocument, pageNumber }) {
  if (!docxUrl || !pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("DOCX URL, PDF document and page number are required.");
  }

  const ocrText = await testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber);
  const docxText = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);

  const ocrLines = ocrText
    .split("\n")
    .map((line) => line.trim())
    .filter((line) => normalizeForMatching(line).length > 25);

  const paragraphs = getTextParagraphs(docxText);

  const matches = [];

  for (const ocrLine of ocrLines) {
    const normalizedOCR = normalizeForMatching(ocrLine);

    for (let i = 0; i < paragraphs.length; i++) {
      const normalizedDOCX = normalizeForMatching(paragraphs[i]);

      if (normalizedDOCX.includes(normalizedOCR)) {
        matches.push({
          ocrLine,
          paragraphIndex: i,
          docxParagraph: paragraphs[i],
        });

        break;
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("PAGE MATCH TEST:", pageNumber);
  console.log("OCR LINES:", ocrLines.length);
  console.log("EXACT MATCHES:", matches.length);
  console.log("MATCH DETAILS:", matches);

  return {
    pageNumber,
    ocrLines: ocrLines.length,
    exactMatches: matches.length,
    matches,
  };
}




export async function testCleanPageBoundaries({
  docxUrl,
  pdfDocument,
  pageNumber,
}) {
  const result = await testDocxPageMatch({
    docxUrl,
    pdfDocument,
    pageNumber,
  });

  const indexes = [
    ...new Set(result.matches.map((match) => match.paragraphIndex)),
  ].sort((a, b) => a - b);

  if (result.exactMatches < 5 || indexes.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("Not enough matches to identify this page.");
  }

  const docxText = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);
  const paragraphs = getTextParagraphs(docxText);

  const firstIndex = indexes[0];
  const lastIndex = indexes[indexes.length - 1];

  console.log("PAGE:", pageNumber);
  console.log("FIRST PARAGRAPH:", firstIndex);
  console.log("LAST PARAGRAPH:", lastIndex);

  console.log("FIRST PARAGRAPH TEXT:", paragraphs[firstIndex]);
  console.log("LAST PARAGRAPH TEXT:", paragraphs[lastIndex]);

  return {
    firstIndex,
    lastIndex,
    firstParagraph: paragraphs[firstIndex],
    lastParagraph: paragraphs[lastIndex],
  };
}



function normalizeWithPositions(text) {
  const characters = [];
  const positions = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const originalCharacter = text[i];

    const normalized = originalCharacter
      .normalize("NFC")
      .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, "")
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]/gu, "")
      .toLowerCase();

    for (const character of normalized) {
      characters.push(character);
      positions.push(i);
    }
  }

  return {
    normalized: characters.join(""),
    positions,
  };
}

const cleanPageTextCache = new WeakMap();

export async function extractVerifiedDocxPage({
  docxUrl,
  pdfDocument,
  pageNumber,
}) {
  if (!docxUrl || !pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("PDF, DOCX and page number are required.");
  }

  let documentCache = cleanPageTextCache.get(pdfDocument);

  if (!documentCache) {
    documentCache = new Map();
    cleanPageTextCache.set(pdfDocument, documentCache);
  }

  const cacheKey = `${docxUrl}:${pageNumber}`;

  if (!documentCache.has(cacheKey)) {
    const request = (async () => {
      const matchResult = await testDocxPageMatch({
        docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });

      const {
        matches,
        ocrLines,
        exactMatches,
      } = matchResult;

      if (
        exactMatches < 10 ||
        exactMatches / ocrLines < 0.7
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "Page matching confidence is too low. Reading stopped."
        );
      }

      const docxText = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);
      const paragraphs = getTextParagraphs(docxText);

      const indexes = matches.map(
        (match) => match.paragraphIndex
      );

      // Matches should progress forward through the DOCX.
      for (let i = 1; i < indexes.length; i++) {
        if (indexes[i] < indexes[i - 1]) {
          throw new Error(
            "DOCX matches are out of order. Reading stopped."
          );
        }
      }

      const firstMatch = matches[0];
      const lastMatch = matches[matches.length - 1];

      const firstIndex = firstMatch.paragraphIndex;
      const lastIndex = lastMatch.paragraphIndex;

      const selectedParagraphs = paragraphs.slice(
        firstIndex,
        lastIndex + 1
      );

      const combinedText = selectedParagraphs.join("\n\n");
      const mapped = normalizeWithPositions(combinedText);

      const normalizedStart = normalizeForMatching(
        firstMatch.ocrLine
      );

      const normalizedEnd = normalizeForMatching(
        lastMatch.ocrLine
      );

      const startPosition = mapped.normalized.indexOf(
        normalizedStart
      );

      const endPosition = mapped.normalized.lastIndexOf(
        normalizedEnd
      );

      if (
        startPosition === -1 ||
        endPosition === -1 ||
        endPosition < startPosition
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "Unable to identify clean page boundaries."
        );
      }

      const originalStart = mapped.positions[startPosition];

      const endCharacterIndex =
        endPosition + normalizedEnd.length - 1;

      const originalEnd =
        mapped.positions[endCharacterIndex] + 1;

      const cleanText = combinedText
        .slice(originalStart, originalEnd)
        .trim();

      if (!cleanText) {
        throw new Error("Clean page text is empty.");
      }

      return {
        pageNumber,
        text: cleanText,
        exactMatches,
        ocrLines,
        firstIndex,
        lastIndex,
      };
    })();

    documentCache.set(cacheKey, request);
  }

  try {
    return await documentCache.get(cacheKey);
  } catch (err) {
    documentCache.delete(cacheKey);
    throw err;
  }
}

const FlipPage = forwardRef(function FlipPage(
  { pdfDocument, number, shouldRender },
  ref
) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pdfDocument || !shouldRender) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let renderTask;

    async function renderPage() {
      try {
        const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(number);

        if (cancelled) return;

        const canvas = canvasRef.current;
        if (!canvas) return;

        const viewport = pdfPage.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

        canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
        canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

        renderTask = pdfPage.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        });

        await renderTask.promise;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error?.name !== "RenderingCancelledException") {
          console.error("Page rendering failed:", error);
        }
      }
    }

    renderPage();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      renderTask?.cancel();
    };
  }, [pdfDocument, number, shouldRender]);

  return (
    <div className="flip-page" ref={ref}>
      <div className="flip-page-content">
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
      </div>
      <span className="flip-page-number">{number}</span>
    </div>
  );
});

  async function handleSpeakPage() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      alert("Text-to-Speech is not supported in this browser.");
      return;
    }

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    // Resume from paused position.
    // if (synth.paused) {
    //   synth.resume();
    //   setTtsStatus("playing");
    //   return;
    // }


    if (ttsStatus === "paused") {
      console.log("RESUME REQUEST: ", {
        paused: synth.paused,
        speaking: synth.speaking,
        pending: synth.pending,
      })

      if (synth.paused) {
        synth.resume();
        setTtsStatus("playing");
      }
      else {
        console.warn("Resume requested, but browser has no paused speech.")
      }

      return;
    }
    // Avoid restarting speech when already playing.
    if (synth.speaking || ttsStatus === "loading") {
      return;
    }

    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

    try {
      setTtsStatus("loading");

      // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;
      // const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
      // const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

      // console.table(
      //   textContent.items.slice(0, 30).map((item, index) => ({
      //     index,
      //     text: item.str,
      //     fontName: item.fontName,
      //     hasEOL: item.hasEOL,
      //   }))
      // )

      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });


      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      // const pageText = textContent.items
      //   .map((item) => item.str || "")
      //   .join(" ")
      //   .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      //   .trim();


      if (!pageText) {
        setTtsStatus("idle");
        alert("No readable text found on this page.");
        return;
      }

      console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("TTS TEXT LENGTH: ", pageText.length);
      console.log("TTS TEXT:", pageText);

      // Divide text into smaller pieces.
      const maxChunkLength = 200;
      const chunks = [];
      let remainingText = pageText;

      while (remainingText.length > 0) {
        if (remainingText.length <= maxChunkLength) {
          chunks.push(remainingText);
          break;
        }

        let splitAt = remainingText.lastIndexOf(
          " ",
          maxChunkLength
        );

        if (splitAt <= 0) {
          splitAt = maxChunkLength;
        }

        const chunk = remainingText.slice(0, splitAt).trim();

        if (chunk) {
          chunks.push(chunk);
        }

        remainingText = remainingText.slice(splitAt).trim();
      }

      console.log("TOTAL TTS CHUNKS:", chunks.length);

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );


      let chunkIndex = 0;

      const speakNextChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
          return;
        }

        if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
          setTtsStatus("idle");
          console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED");
          return;
        }


        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          chunks[chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) {
          utterance.voice = voice;
        }

        utterance.onstart = () => {
          if (sessionId === speechSessionRef.current) {
            setTtsStatus("playing");
          }
        };

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          chunkIndex += 1;

          speakNextChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        console.log(
          `READING CHUNK ${chunkIndex + 1}/${chunks.length}`
        );

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      speakNextChunk();
    } catch (error) {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.error("TTS failed:", error);
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      alert(error.message || "Unable to rea this page aloud.")
    }
  }

  // function handlePauseSpeech() {
  //   if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

  //   window.speechSynthesis.pause();
  //   setTtsStatus("paused");
  // }

  function handleStopSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    // Invalidate pending and future chunks.
    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("idle");
  }

  function handlePauseSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    synth.pause();

    console.log("PAUSE REQUEST: ", {
      paused: synth.paused,
      speaking: synth.speaking,
      pending: synth.pending,
    })

    setTtsStatus("paused");
  }



  async function handleTranslate() {
    if (!extractedText.trim()) {
      alert("Please extract the PDF text first.");
      return;
    }

    if (selectedLanguage === "en") {
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setTranslating(true);
      setTranslationError("");

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(extractedText);

      // const testText =
      //   "At the core of quantum information processing is the quantum bit, or qubit.";

      // const testText = "The sun rises in the east.";

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(testText);

      setTranslationProgress("Preparing translation...");

      const translated = await translateToHindi(
        extractedText,
        (completed, total) => {
          setTranslationProgress(
            `Translating ${completed} of ${total} chunks...`
          );
        }
      );

      setTranslatedText(translated);

      console.log("HINDI TRANSLATION:", translated);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Translation failed:", error);

      setTranslationError(
        error?.message || "Unable to translate this page."
      );

    } finally {
      setTranslating(false);
      setTranslationProgress("");
    }
  }


  if (productLoading) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <p>Loading your book...</p>
      </main>
    )
  }


  if (!product) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <h1>Book not found</h1>
        <Link to="/">Return to SKS Dream</Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="reader-page">
      <header className="reader-header">
        <Link to="/" className="reader-back">
          ← Back to SKS Dream
        </Link>
        <span className="reader-brand">SKS Dream</span>
      </header>

      <section className="reader-heading">
        <p className="reader-eyebrow">DIGITAL LIBRARY</p>
        <h1>{product.name}</h1>
        <p>Settle in, turn the pages, and enjoy your reading experience.</p>
      </section>

      {loading && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="status">
          Preparing your book…
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="alert">
          <p>{error}</p>
          <Link to="/">Return to products</Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* {!loading && !error && pages.length > 0 && ( */}
      {!loading && !error && pdfDocument && totalPages > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* Zooom Effects */}

          <nav
            className="reader-zoom-toolbar"
            aria-label="Reading zoom controls"
          >
            <span className="reader-zoom-label">Reading View</span>

            <div className="reader-zoom-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomOut}
                disabled={zoom <= 75}
                aria-label="Zoom out"
              >
                -
              </button>

              <span className="reader-zoom-level">{zoom}%</span>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomIn}
                disabled={zoom >= 200}
                aria-label="Zoom in"
              >
                +
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="reader-zoom-reset"
                onClick={resetZoom}
              >
                Reset
              </button>
            </div>
          </nav>

          <div className="reader-book-viewport">
            <div className="reader-book-zoom" style={{ zoom: zoom / 100 }}>
              <section className="book-stage" aria-label="Interactive book">
                <HTMLFlipBook
                  ref={flipBookRef}
                  width={380}
                  height={520}
                  size="stretch"
                  minWidth={260}
                  maxWidth={760}
                  minHeight={360}
                  maxHeight={620}
                  showCover
                  usePortrait
                  mobileScrollSupport
                  drawShadow
                  flippingTime={650}
                  maxShadowOpacity={0.45}
                  onFlip={(event) => {
                    // window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                    handleStopSpeech();

                    setCurrentPage(event.data);
                    setExtractedText("");
                    setTranslatedText("");
                    setTranslationError("");
                  }}
                  className="book-flip"
                >
                  {/* {pages.map((page, index) => (
                    <FlipPage key={index} page={page} number={index + 1} />
                  ))} */}

                  {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => (
                    <FlipPage
                      key={`${product.id}-${index}`}
                      pdfDocument={pdfDocument}
                      number={index + 1}
                      shouldRender={Math.abs(index - currentPage) <= 3}
                    />
                  ))}

                </HTMLFlipBook>
              </section>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "20px 0" }} >

            {product?.docxUrl && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleReadDocx}
                disabled={docxLoading}
                style={{ marginRight: "10px" }}
              >
                {docxLoading ? "Loading DOCX..." : "Read Clean DOCX Text"}
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={extractCurrentPageText}
              disabled={extracting}
            >
              {extracting ? "Extracting..." : "Extract Page Text"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestOCR}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Marathi OCR
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestDocxMatch}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              TEST DOCX Matching
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestPageBoundaries}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Page Boundaries
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestCleanPage}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Clean Page
            </button>

            <div style={{ marginTop: "15px" }}>

              <label htmlFor="pdf-language">
                Select Language:{" "}
              </label>

              <select id="pdf-language"
                value={selectedLanguage}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setSelectedLanguage(event.target.value)
                  setTranslatedText("");
                  setTranslationError("");
                }}
              >
                <option value="en">English</option>
                <option value="hi">Hindi</option>

              </select>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTranslate}
                disabled={translating || extracting || !extractedText}
                style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
              >
                {translating ? "Translating..." : "Translate"}
              </button>

              {translating && translationProgress && (
                <p
                  style={{
                    marginTop: "10px",
                    fontSize: "14px",
                    color: "#555",
                  }}
                >
                  {translationProgress}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {extractedText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "300px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Extracted PDF Text</h3>

                {/* <p>{extractedText}</p> */}
                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >{
                    selectedLanguage === "hi" && translatedText
                      ? translatedText
                      : extractedText
                  }</p>

                {translationError && (
                  <p
                    style={{ color: "red", marginTop: "10px" }}
                  >{translationError}</p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* DOCX extraction error */}
            {docxError && (
              <p role="alert" style={{ color: "red" }}>
                {docxError}
              </p>
            )}

            {/* Clean DOCX text display */}
            {docxText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "350px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Clean DOCX Text</h3>

                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >
                  {docxText}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}


          </div>

          <div className="reader-controls">

            <select
              value={ttsLanguage}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setTtsLanguage(event.target.value);
                setSelectedVoice("");
              }}
            >
              <option value="en-IN">English</option>
              <option value="hi-IN">Hindi</option>
              <option value="mr-IN">Marathi</option>
              <option value="sa-IN">Sanskrit</option>
            </select>

            <select
              value={selectedVoice}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setSelectedVoice(event.target.value);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">Default Voice</option>

              {ttsVoices
                .filter((voice) =>
                  voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
                    ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
                  )
                )
                .map((voice) => (
                  <option
                    key={`${voice.name}-${voice.lang}`}
                    value={voice.name}
                  >
                    {voice.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={ttsSpeed}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setTtsSpeed(Number(event.target.value))
              }
            >
              <option value={0.75}>0.75× Slow</option>
              <option value={1}>1× Normal</option>
              <option value={1.25}>1.25× Fast</option>
              <option value={1.5}>1.5× Faster</option>
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSpeakPage}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "loading"}
            >
              {ttsStatus === "paused"
                ? "▶ Resume"
                : ttsStatus === "playing"
                  ? "🔊 Playing"
                  : "🔊 Listen"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handlePauseSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus !== "playing"}
            >
              ⏸ Pause
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStopSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "idle"}
            >
              ⏹ Stop
            </button>

          </div>

          <nav className="reader-controls" aria-label="Book navigation">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goPrevious}
              disabled={currentPage === 0}
            >
              ← Previous
            </button>

            <span>
              {/* Page {currentPage + 1} of {pages.length} */}
              Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goNext}
              // disabled={currentPage >= pages.length - 1}
              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
            >
              Next →
            </button>
          </nav>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

const FlipPage = forwardRef(function FlipPage(
  { pdfDocument, number, shouldRender },
  ref
) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pdfDocument || !shouldRender) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let renderTask;

    async function renderPage() {
      try {
        const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(number);

        if (cancelled) return;

        const canvas = canvasRef.current;
        if (!canvas) return;

        const viewport = pdfPage.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

        canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
        canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

        renderTask = pdfPage.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        });

        await renderTask.promise;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error?.name !== "RenderingCancelledException") {
          console.error("Page rendering failed:", error);
        }
      }
    }

    renderPage();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      renderTask?.cancel();
    };
  }, [pdfDocument, number, shouldRender]);

  return (
    <div className="flip-page" ref={ref}>
      <div className="flip-page-content">
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
      </div>
      <span className="flip-page-number">{number}</span>
    </div>
  );
});

  const { id } = useParams();
  const flipBookRef = useRef(null);
  const speechSessionRef = useRef(0);

    pageNumber: null,
    chunks: [],

    status: "idle",
  })

  const autoFlipRef = useRef(false);

  const readingQueueRef = useRef([]);

  const pagePreparationCacheRef = useRef(new Map());


  const [pages, setPages] = useState([]);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(100);
  const [pdfDocument, setPdfDocument] = useState(null);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0);
  const [extractedText, setExtractedText] = useState("");
  const [extracting, setExtracting] = useState(false);

  const [docxText, setDocxText] = useState("");
  const [docxLoading, setDocxLoading] = useState(false);
  const [docxError, setDocxError] = useState("");

  const [selectedLanguage, setSelectedLanguage] = useState("en");

  const [translating, setTranslating] = useState(false);
  const [translationError, setTranslationError] = useState("");

  function zoomIn() {
    setZoom((current) => Math.min(current + 25, 200));

    setZoom((current) => Math.max(current - 25, 75));
  }

  function resetZoom() {
    setZoom(100);

    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadProduct() {
      setProductLoading(true);

      try {
        const book = await getBookById(id);

        if (!cancelled) {
          setProduct(book);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Book loading failed:", error);

        if (!cancelled) {
          setProduct(null);
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setProductLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadProduct();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [id]);

    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

          name: voice.name,
          lang: voice.lang,
        }))
      );
    };

    loadVoices();

    window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener(
      "voiceschanged",
      loadVoices
    );

    return () => {
      window.speechSynthesis.removeEventListener(
        "voiceschanged",
        loadVoices
      );
    };

      setLoading(true);

      setPages([]);
      setCurrentPage(0);
      setPdfDocument(null);
      setTotalPages(0);
      pagePreparationCacheRef.current.clear();

      if (!product?.pdfUrl) {
        setError("This book does not have a PDF available yet.");

        return;
      }

      try {

        loadingTask = pdfjsLib.getDocument({ url: pdfUrl });
        const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;

        if (cancelled) return;

        setPdfDocument(pdf);

      } catch (err) {
        if (cancelled || err?.name === "AbortException") {
          return;
        }

        console.error("PDF loading failed:", err);

        setError(
          "We couldn't open this PDF. Check the file path and try again."
        );

      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }




    loadPdf();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      loadingTask?.destroy();
    };
  }, [product?.pdfUrl]);

  function goPrevious() {

  async function handleReadDocx() {

    if (!product?.docxUrl) return;


    try {
      setDocxLoading(true);
      setDocxError("")

      const text = await extractDocxText(product.docxUrl);
      setDocxText(text);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("DOCX extraction failed:", error);

    } finally {

  }

  async function handleTestOCR() {

  async function extractCurrentPageText() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    try {
      setExtracting(true);
      setExtractedText("");
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");

      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const cleanText = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl: product?.docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });

      setExtractedText(cleanText);

      console.log("CLEAN PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("CLEAN TEXT:", cleanText);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Clean text extraction failed:", error);
      alert(error.message || "Unable to extract page text.");
    } finally {
      setExtracting(false);
    }
  }

  // async function handleSpeakPage() {
  //   if (!pdfDocument) {
  //     alert("PDF is not ready yet.");
