  function getVisiblePageNumbers() {
    const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

    if (!flipInstance || !totalPages) {
      return [];
    }

    const currentIndex = flipInstance.getCurrentPageIndex();
    const orientation = flipInstance.getOrientation();

    const firstPage = currentIndex + 1;

    if (orientation === "portrait") {
      return [firstPage];
    }

    const secondPage = firstPage + 1;

    if (secondPage <= totalPages) {
      return [firstPage, secondPage];
    }

    return [firstPage];
  }


  function flipToNextReadingPages() {
    const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

    if (!flipInstance) return false;

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) return false;

    const lastVisiblePage = visiblePages[visiblePages.length - 1];

    if (lastVisiblePage >= totalPages) {
      return false;
    }

    autoFlipRef.current = true;

    flipInstance.flipNext();

    return true;

  }

  function preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber) {
    if (!pdfDocument || !product?.docxUrl) {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error("PDF or DOCX is not ready.")
      )
    }


    const cache = pagePreparationCacheRef.current;

    // Already prepared or currently preperting 

    if (cache.has(pageNumber)) {
      return cache.get(pageNumber);
    }

    console.log("BACKGROUND PREPARATION START:", pageNumber);

    const preparationPromise = getReadablePageText({
      docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
      pdfDocument,
      pageNumber,
    })
      .then((text) => {
        if (!text?.trim()) {
          throw new Error (
            `No readable text found on Page ${pageNumber}`
          )
        }

        console.log("BACKGROUND PREPARATION READY:", pageNumber);

        return text.trim();
    }).catch((error)=> {
      //Failed results should not stay cached.

      if (cache.get(pageNumber) === preparationPromise) {
        cache.delete(pageNumber);
      }

      throw error;
    })

    cache.set(pageNumber, preparationPromise);

    return preparationPromise;

  }


  async function readNextVisiblePage(pageNumber, sessionId) {

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    try {

      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      setTtsStatus("loading");

      // const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
      //   docxUrl: product?.docxUrl,
      //   pdfDocument,
      //   pageNumber,
      // });

      console.log("Using PREPARE PAGE:", pageNumber);

      const pageText = await preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber)

      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      if (!pageText?.trim()) {
        throw new Error(`No readable text found on page ${pageNumber}`);
      }


      const chunks = [];
      const maxChunkLength = 200;
      let remainingText = pageText.trim();

      while (remainingText.length > 0) {
        if (remainingText.length <= maxChunkLength) {
          chunks.push(remainingText);
          break;
        }

        let splitAt = remainingText.lastIndexOf(" ", maxChunkLength);

        if (splitAt <= 0) splitAt = maxChunkLength;

        const chunk = remainingText.slice(0, splitAt).trim();

        if (chunk) chunks.push(chunk);

        remainingText = remainingText.slice(splitAt).trim();
      }

      readingProgressRef.current = {
        pageNumber,
        chunks,
        chunkIndex: 0,
        status: "loading",
      };

      const upcomingPage = readingQueueRef.current[0];

      if (upcomingPage) {
        preparePageForSpeech(upcomingPage).catch((error)=> {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.warn(
            "UPCOMING PAGE PREPARATION FAILED:",
            upcomingPage,
            error
          )

        })
      }


      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );

      let chunkIndex = 0;

      const speakNextChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

        readingProgressRef.current.chunkIndex = chunkIndex;

        if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
          console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED:", pageNumber);

          const nextPageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

          if (nextPageNumber) {
            readNextVisiblePage(nextPageNumber, sessionId);
          } else {
            readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
            setTtsStatus("idle");
            console.log("VISIBLE PAGES READING COMPLETED");
          }

          return;
        }

        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          chunks[chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

        utterance.onstart = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          readingProgressRef.current.status = "playing";
          setTtsStatus("playing");
        };

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          chunkIndex += 1;
          speakNextChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
          readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
      speakNextChunk();

    } catch (error) {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.error("Next page reading failed:", error);
      readingQueueRef.current = [];
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
    }
  }

  async function handleSpeakPage() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      alert("Text-to-Speech is not supported in this browser.");
      return;
    }

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    // Resume from paused position.
    // if (synth.paused) {
    //   synth.resume();
    //   setTtsStatus("playing");
    //   return;
    // }


    if (ttsStatus === "paused") {
      // console.log("RESUME REQUEST: ", {
      //   paused: synth.paused,
      //   speaking: synth.speaking,
      //   pending: synth.pending,
      // })

      const progress = readingProgressRef.current;

      if (!progress.chunks.length) {
        setTtsStatus("idle");
        return;
      }

      // if (synth.paused) {
      //   synth.resume();
      //   setTtsStatus("playing");
      // }
      // else {
      //   console.warn("Resume requested, but browser has no paused speech.")
      // }

      const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||

        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );

      progress.status = "playing";
      setTtsStatus("playing");

      const resumeChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

        if (progress.chunkIndex >= progress.chunks.length) {
          progress.status = "idle";
          setTtsStatus("idle");
          return;
        }

        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          progress.chunks[progress.chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          progress.chunkIndex += 1;
          resumeChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.error("Resume TTS error:", event.error);
          progress.status = "idle";
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      resumeChunk();

      return;
    }
    // Avoid restarting speech when already playing.
    if (synth.speaking || ttsStatus === "loading") {
      return;
    }

    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) {
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      return;
    }

    readingQueueRef.current = [...visiblePages];

    try {
      setTtsStatus("loading");

      // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;
      // const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
      // const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

      // console.table(
      //   textContent.items.slice(0, 30).map((item, index) => ({
      //     index,
      //     text: item.str,
      //     fontName: item.fontName,
      //     hasEOL: item.hasEOL,
      //   }))
      // )

      // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const pageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

      const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });


      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      // const pageText = textContent.items
      //   .map((item) => item.str || "")
      //   .join(" ")
      //   .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      //   .trim();


      if (!pageText) {
        setTtsStatus("idle");
        alert("No readable text found on this page.");
        return;
      }

      console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("TTS TEXT LENGTH: ", pageText.length);
      console.log("TTS TEXT:", pageText);

      // Divide text into smaller pieces.
      const maxChunkLength = 200;
      const chunks = [];
      let remainingText = pageText;

      while (remainingText.length > 0) {
        if (remainingText.length <= maxChunkLength) {
          chunks.push(remainingText);
          break;
        }

        let splitAt = remainingText.lastIndexOf(
          " ",
          maxChunkLength
        );

        if (splitAt <= 0) {
          splitAt = maxChunkLength;
        }

        const chunk = remainingText.slice(0, splitAt).trim();

        if (chunk) {
          chunks.push(chunk);
        }

        remainingText = remainingText.slice(splitAt).trim();
      }

      console.log("TOTAL TTS CHUNKS:", chunks.length);

      readingProgressRef.current = {
        pageNumber,
        chunks,
        chunkIndex: 0,
        status: "loading",
      }

      // Prepare the next visible page while this page is speaking.

      // const nextPageToPrepare = readingProgressRef.current[0];

      const nextPageToPrepare = readingQueueRef.current[0];

      if (nextPageToPrepare) {
        preparePageForSpeech(nextPageToPrepare).catch((error)=> {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.warn(
            "NEXT PAGE PREPARATION FAILED:",
            nextPageToPrepare,
            error
          )
        })
      }

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );


      let chunkIndex = 0;

      const speakNextChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
          return;
        }

        readingProgressRef.current.chunkIndex = chunkIndex;

        // if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
        //   setTtsStatus("idle");
        //   console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED");
        //   return;
        // }

        if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {

          console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED:", pageNumber);

          const nextPageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

          if (!nextPageNumber) {
            readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
            setTtsStatus("idle");
            console.log("VISIBLE PAGE READING COMPLETED");
            return;
          }

          console.log("STARTING NEXT VISIBLE PAGE:", nextPageNumber);

          readNextVisiblePage(nextPageNumber, sessionId);
          return;

        }


        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          chunks[chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) {
          utterance.voice = voice;
        }

        utterance.onstart = () => {
          if (sessionId === speechSessionRef.current) {
            readingProgressRef.current.status = "playing";
            setTtsStatus("playing");
          }
        };

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          chunkIndex += 1;

          speakNextChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) {
            return;
          }

          console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        console.log(
          `READING CHUNK ${chunkIndex + 1}/${chunks.length}`
        );

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      speakNextChunk();
    } catch (error) {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.error("TTS failed:", error);
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      alert(error.message || "Unable to rea this page aloud.")
    }
  }

  // function handlePauseSpeech() {
  //   if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

  //   window.speechSynthesis.pause();
  //   setTtsStatus("paused");
  // }

  function handleStopSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    // Invalidate pending and future chunks.
    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    readingQueueRef.current = [];

    readingProgressRef.current = {
      pageNumber: null,
      chunks: [],
      chunkIndex: 0,
      status: "idle",
    }

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("idle");
  }

  // function handlePauseSpeech() {
  //   if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

  //   const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

  //   synth.pause();

  //   console.log("PAUSE REQUEST: ", {
  //     paused: synth.paused,
  //     speaking: synth.speaking,
  //     pending: synth.pending,
  //   })

  //   setTtsStatus("paused");
  // }


  function handlePauseSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    if (readingProgressRef.current.status !== "playing") return;

    // Cancle the current speech session, but preserve reading progress.

    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    readingProgressRef.current.status = "paused";

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("paused");

  }


  async function handleTranslate() {
    if (!extractedText.trim()) {
      alert("Please extract the PDF text first.");
      return;
    }

    if (selectedLanguage === "en") {
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setTranslating(true);
      setTranslationError("");

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(extractedText);

      // const testText =
      //   "At the core of quantum information processing is the quantum bit, or qubit.";

      // const testText = "The sun rises in the east.";

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(testText);

      setTranslationProgress("Preparing translation...");

      const translated = await translateToHindi(
        extractedText,
        (completed, total) => {
          setTranslationProgress(
            `Translating ${completed} of ${total} chunks...`
          );
        }
      );

      setTranslatedText(translated);

      console.log("HINDI TRANSLATION:", translated);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Translation failed:", error);

      setTranslationError(
        error?.message || "Unable to translate this page."
      );

    } finally {
      setTranslating(false);
      setTranslationProgress("");
    }
  }


  if (productLoading) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <p>Loading your book...</p>
      </main>
    )
  }


  if (!product) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <h1>Book not found</h1>
        <Link to="/">Return to SKS Dream</Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="reader-page">
      <header className="reader-header">
        <Link to="/" className="reader-back">
          ← Back to SKS Dream
        </Link>
        <span className="reader-brand">SKS Dream</span>
      </header>

      <section className="reader-heading">
        <p className="reader-eyebrow">DIGITAL LIBRARY</p>
        <h1>{product.name}</h1>
        <p>Settle in, turn the pages, and enjoy your reading experience.</p>
      </section>

      {loading && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="status">
          Preparing your book…
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="alert">
          <p>{error}</p>
          <Link to="/">Return to products</Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* {!loading && !error && pages.length > 0 && ( */}
      {!loading && !error && pdfDocument && totalPages > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* Zooom Effects */}

          <nav
            className="reader-zoom-toolbar"
            aria-label="Reading zoom controls"
          >
            <span className="reader-zoom-label">Reading View</span>

            <div className="reader-zoom-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomOut}
                disabled={zoom <= 75}
                aria-label="Zoom out"
              >
                -
              </button>

              <span className="reader-zoom-level">{zoom}%</span>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomIn}
                disabled={zoom >= 200}
                aria-label="Zoom in"
              >
                +
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="reader-zoom-reset"
                onClick={resetZoom}
              >
                Reset
              </button>
            </div>
          </nav>

          <div className="reader-book-viewport">
            <div className="reader-book-zoom" style={{ zoom: zoom / 100 }}>
              <section className="book-stage" aria-label="Interactive book">
                <HTMLFlipBook
                  ref={flipBookRef}
                  width={380}
                  height={520}
                  size="stretch"
                  minWidth={260}
                  maxWidth={760}
                  minHeight={360}
                  maxHeight={620}
                  showCover
                  usePortrait
                  mobileScrollSupport
                  drawShadow
                  flippingTime={650}
                  maxShadowOpacity={0.45}
                  onFlip={(event) => {
                    // window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                    // handleStopSpeech();

                    // setCurrentPage(event.data);

                    const isAutomaticFlip = autoFlipRef.current;

                    autoFlipRef.current = false;

                    if (!isAutomaticFlip) {
                      handleStopSpeech();
                    }

                    setCurrentPage(event.data);

                    console.log("FLIP SOURCE:", isAutomaticFlip ? "automatic" : "manual");

                    const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

                    console.log("FLIPBOOK PAGE INFO:", {
                      eventPageIndex: event.data,
                      currentPageIndex: flipInstance?.getCurrentPageIndex(),
                      orientation: flipInstance?.getOrientation(),
                      pageCount: flipInstance?.getPageCount(),
                    });

                    console.log("VISIBLE PDF PAGES:", getVisiblePageNumbers());

                    setExtractedText("");
                    setTranslatedText("");
                    setTranslationError("");
                  }}
                  className="book-flip"
                >
                  {/* {pages.map((page, index) => (
                    <FlipPage key={index} page={page} number={index + 1} />
                  ))} */}

                  {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => (
                    <FlipPage
                      key={`${product.id}-${index}`}
                      pdfDocument={pdfDocument}
                      number={index + 1}
                      shouldRender={Math.abs(index - currentPage) <= 3}
                    />
                  ))}

                </HTMLFlipBook>
              </section>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "20px 0" }} >

            {product?.docxUrl && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleReadDocx}
                disabled={docxLoading}
                style={{ marginRight: "10px" }}
              >
                {docxLoading ? "Loading DOCX..." : "Read Clean DOCX Text"}
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={extractCurrentPageText}
              disabled={extracting}
            >
              {extracting ? "Extracting..." : "Extract Page Text"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestOCR}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Marathi OCR
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestDocxMatch}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              TEST DOCX Matching
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestPageBoundaries}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Page Boundaries
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestCleanPage}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Clean Page
            </button>

            <div style={{ marginTop: "15px" }}>

              <label htmlFor="pdf-language">
                Select Language:{" "}
              </label>

              <select id="pdf-language"
                value={selectedLanguage}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setSelectedLanguage(event.target.value)
                  setTranslatedText("");
                  setTranslationError("");
                }}
              >
                <option value="en">English</option>
                <option value="hi">Hindi</option>

              </select>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTranslate}
                disabled={translating || extracting || !extractedText}
                style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
              >
                {translating ? "Translating..." : "Translate"}
              </button>

              {translating && translationProgress && (
                <p
                  style={{
                    marginTop: "10px",
                    fontSize: "14px",
                    color: "#555",
                  }}
                >
                  {translationProgress}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {extractedText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "300px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Extracted PDF Text</h3>

                {/* <p>{extractedText}</p> */}
                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >{
                    selectedLanguage === "hi" && translatedText
                      ? translatedText
                      : extractedText
                  }</p>

                {translationError && (
                  <p
                    style={{ color: "red", marginTop: "10px" }}
                  >{translationError}</p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* DOCX extraction error */}
            {docxError && (
              <p role="alert" style={{ color: "red" }}>
                {docxError}
              </p>
            )}

            {/* Clean DOCX text display */}
            {docxText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "350px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Clean DOCX Text</h3>

                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >
                  {docxText}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}


          </div>

          <div className="reader-controls">

            <select
              value={ttsLanguage}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setTtsLanguage(event.target.value);
                setSelectedVoice("");
              }}
            >
              <option value="en-IN">English</option>
              <option value="hi-IN">Hindi</option>
              <option value="mr-IN">Marathi</option>
              <option value="sa-IN">Sanskrit</option>
            </select>

            <select
              value={selectedVoice}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setSelectedVoice(event.target.value);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">Default Voice</option>

              {ttsVoices
                .filter((voice) =>
                  voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
                    ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
                  )
                )
                .map((voice) => (
                  <option
                    key={`${voice.name}-${voice.lang}`}
                    value={voice.name}
                  >
                    {voice.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={ttsSpeed}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setTtsSpeed(Number(event.target.value))
              }
            >
              <option value={0.75}>0.75× Slow</option>
              <option value={1}>1× Normal</option>
              <option value={1.25}>1.25× Fast</option>
              <option value={1.5}>1.5× Faster</option>
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSpeakPage}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "loading"}
            >
              {ttsStatus === "paused"
                ? "▶ Resume"
                : ttsStatus === "playing"
                  ? "🔊 Playing"
                  : "🔊 Listen"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handlePauseSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus !== "playing"}
            >
              ⏸ Pause
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStopSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "idle"}
            >
              ⏹ Stop
            </button>

          </div>

          <nav className="reader-controls" aria-label="Book navigation">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goPrevious}
              disabled={currentPage === 0}
            >
              ← Previous
            </button>

            <span>
              {/* Page {currentPage + 1} of {pages.length} */}
              Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goNext}
              // disabled={currentPage >= pages.length - 1}
              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
            >
              Next →
            </button>
          </nav>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

const FlipPage = forwardRef(function FlipPage(
  { pdfDocument, number, shouldRender },
  ref
) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pdfDocument || !shouldRender) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let renderTask;

    async function renderPage() {
      try {
        const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(number);

        if (cancelled) return;

        const canvas = canvasRef.current;
        if (!canvas) return;

        const viewport = pdfPage.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

        canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
        canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

        renderTask = pdfPage.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        });

        await renderTask.promise;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error?.name !== "RenderingCancelledException") {
          console.error("Page rendering failed:", error);
        }
      }
    }

    renderPage();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      renderTask?.cancel();
    };
  }, [pdfDocument, number, shouldRender]);

  return (
    <div className="flip-page" ref={ref}>
      <div className="flip-page-content">
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
      </div>
      <span className="flip-page-number">{number}</span>
    </div>
  );
});

  async function extractCurrentPageText() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    try {
      setExtracting(true);
      setExtractedText("");
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");

      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const cleanText = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl: product?.docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });

      setExtractedText(cleanText);

      console.log("CLEAN PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("CLEAN TEXT:", cleanText);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Clean text extraction failed:", error);
      alert(error.message || "Unable to extract page text.");
    } finally {
      setExtracting(false);
    }
  }

  // function handleStopSpeech() {

  function getVisiblePageNumbers() {
    const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

    if (!flipInstance || !totalPages) {
      return [];
    }

    const currentIndex = flipInstance.getCurrentPageIndex();
    const orientation = flipInstance.getOrientation();

    if (currentIndex < 0 || currentIndex >= totalPages) {
      return [];
    }

    const firstPage = currentIndex + 1;


    // Mobile: one page at a time.
    if (orientation === "portrait") {
      return [firstPage];
    }


    // if (secondPage <= totalPages) {
    //   return [firstPage, secondPage];
    // }

    // return [firstPage];


    // Desktop: cover is displayed alon
    if (currentIndex === 0) {
      return [1];
    }

    // Desktop: two-page spread
    const secondPage = firstPage + 1;

    return secondPage <= totalPages
      ? [firstPage, secondPage]
      : [firstPage];

  }


  function flipToNextReadingPages() {
    const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

    if (!flipInstance) return false;

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) return false;

    const lastVisiblePage = visiblePages[visiblePages.length - 1];

    if (lastVisiblePage >= totalPages) {
      return false;
    }

    autoFlipRef.current = true;

    flipInstance.flipNext();

    return true;

  }

  function preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber) {
    if (!pdfDocument || !product?.docxUrl) {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error("PDF or DOCX is not ready.")
      )
    }


    const cache = pagePreparationCacheRef.current;

    // Already prepared or currently preperting 

    if (cache.has(pageNumber)) {
      return cache.get(pageNumber);
    }

    console.log("BACKGROUND PREPARATION START:", pageNumber);

    const preparationPromise = getReadablePageText({
      docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
      pdfDocument,
      pageNumber,
    })
      .then((text) => {
        if (!text?.trim()) {
          throw new Error(
            `No readable text found on Page ${pageNumber}`
          )
        }

        console.log("BACKGROUND PREPARATION READY:", pageNumber);

        return text.trim();
      }).catch((error) => {
        //Failed results should not stay cached.

        if (cache.get(pageNumber) === preparationPromise) {
          cache.delete(pageNumber);
        }

        throw error;
      })

    cache.set(pageNumber, preparationPromise);

    return preparationPromise;

  }

  function preloadUpcomingPage(pageNumber, sessionId) {

    if (
      !pageNumber ||
      pageNumber < 1 ||
      pageNumber > totalPages
    ) {
      return;
    }

    preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber).catch((error) => {

      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.warn(
        "BACKGROUND PAGE PREPARATION FAILED:",
        pageNumber,
        error,
      )
    })
  }


  function completeReadingPage(sessionId) {
    if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

    const nextPageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

    if (nextPageNumber) {
      readNextVisiblePage(nextPageNumber, sessionId);
      return;
    }

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) {
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      return;
    }


    const lastVisiblePage = visiblePages[visiblePages.length - 1];

    if (lastVisiblePage >= totalPages) {
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      console.log("BOOK READING COMPLETED");
      return;
    }

    pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = sessionId;

    readingProgressRef.current.status = "loading";
    setTtsStatus("loading");

    const flipped = flipToNextReadingPages();

    if (!flipped) {
      pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = null;
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
    }
  }


  async function readNextVisiblePage(pageNumber, sessionId) {

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    try {

      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      setTtsStatus("loading");

      // const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
      //   docxUrl: product?.docxUrl,
      //   pdfDocument,
      //   pageNumber,
      // });

      console.log("Using PREPARE PAGE:", pageNumber);

      const pageText = await preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber)

      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      if (!pageText?.trim()) {
        throw new Error(`No readable text found on page ${pageNumber}`);
      }


      const chunks = [];
      const maxChunkLength = 200;
      let remainingText = pageText.trim();

      while (remainingText.length > 0) {
        if (remainingText.length <= maxChunkLength) {
          chunks.push(remainingText);
          break;
        }

        let splitAt = remainingText.lastIndexOf(" ", maxChunkLength);

        if (splitAt <= 0) splitAt = maxChunkLength;

        const chunk = remainingText.slice(0, splitAt).trim();

        if (chunk) chunks.push(chunk);

        remainingText = remainingText.slice(splitAt).trim();
      }

      readingProgressRef.current = {
        pageNumber,
        chunks,
        chunkIndex: 0,
        status: "loading",
      };

      const upcomingPage =
        readingQueueRef.current[0] ||
        (pageNumber < totalPages ? pageNumber + 1 : null);

      preloadUpcomingPage(upcomingPage, sessionId);

      // if (upcomingPage) {
      //   preparePageForSpeech(upcomingPage).catch((error) => {
      //     if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      //     console.warn(
      //       "UPCOMING PAGE PREPARATION FAILED:",
      //       upcomingPage,
      //       error
      //     )

      //   })
      // }




      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );

      let chunkIndex = 0;

      const speakNextChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

        readingProgressRef.current.chunkIndex = chunkIndex;

        // if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
        //   console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED:", pageNumber);

        //   const nextPageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

        //   if (nextPageNumber) {
        //     readNextVisiblePage(nextPageNumber, sessionId);
        //   } else {
        //     readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
        //     setTtsStatus("idle");
        //     console.log("VISIBLE PAGES READING COMPLETED");
        //   }

        //   return;
        // }

        if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
          console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED:", pageNumber);

          completeReadingPage(sessionId);
          return;
        }

        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          chunks[chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

        utterance.onstart = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          readingProgressRef.current.status = "playing";
          setTtsStatus("playing");
        };

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          chunkIndex += 1;
          speakNextChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
          readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
      speakNextChunk();

    } catch (error) {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.error("Next page reading failed:", error);
      readingQueueRef.current = [];
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
    }
  }

  async function handleSpeakPage() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      alert("Text-to-Speech is not supported in this browser.");
      return;
    }

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    // Resume from paused position.
    // if (synth.paused) {
    //   synth.resume();
    //   setTtsStatus("playing");
    //   return;
    // }


    if (ttsStatus === "paused") {
      // console.log("RESUME REQUEST: ", {
      //   paused: synth.paused,
      //   speaking: synth.speaking,
      //   pending: synth.pending,
      // })

      const progress = readingProgressRef.current;

      if (!progress.chunks.length) {
        setTtsStatus("idle");
        return;
      }

      // if (synth.paused) {
      //   synth.resume();
      //   setTtsStatus("playing");
      // }
      // else {
      //   console.warn("Resume requested, but browser has no paused speech.")
      // }

      const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
              ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
            )
        ) ||

        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase()
        );

      progress.status = "playing";
      setTtsStatus("playing");

      const resumeChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

        // if (progress.chunkIndex >= progress.chunks.length) {
        //   progress.status = "idle";
        //   setTtsStatus("idle");
        //   return;
        // }

        if (progress.chunkIndex >= progress.chunks.length) {
          console.log("RESUMED PAGE COMPLETED:", progress.pageNumber);

          completeReadingPage(sessionId);
          return;
        }

        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          progress.chunks[progress.chunkIndex]
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          progress.chunkIndex += 1;
          resumeChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.error("Resume TTS error:", event.error);
          progress.status = "idle";
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      resumeChunk();

      return;
    }
    // Avoid restarting speech when already playing.
    if (synth.speaking || ttsStatus === "loading") {
      return;
    }

    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) {
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      return;
    }

    readingQueueRef.current = [...visiblePages];

    const firstPage = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

    readingProgressRef.current.status = "loading";
    setTtsStatus("loading");

    readNextVisiblePage(firstPage, sessionId);

    // try {
    //   setTtsStatus("loading");

      // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;
      // const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
      // const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

      // console.table(
      //   textContent.items.slice(0, 30).map((item, index) => ({
      //     index,
      //     text: item.str,
      //     fontName: item.fontName,
      //     hasEOL: item.hasEOL,
      //   }))
      // )

      // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      // const pageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

      // const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
      //   docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
      //   pdfDocument,
      //   pageNumber,
      // });


      // if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      // const pageText = textContent.items
      //   .map((item) => item.str || "")
      //   .join(" ")
      //   .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      //   .trim();


    //   if (!pageText) {
    //     setTtsStatus("idle");
    //     alert("No readable text found on this page.");
    //     return;
    //   }

    //   console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
    //   console.log("TTS TEXT LENGTH: ", pageText.length);
    //   console.log("TTS TEXT:", pageText);

    //   // Divide text into smaller pieces.

  function handleStopSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    // Invalidate pending and future chunks.
    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = null;
    autoFlipRef.current = false;

    readingQueueRef.current = [];

    readingProgressRef.current = {
      pageNumber: null,
      chunks: [],
      chunkIndex: 0,
      status: "idle",
    }

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("idle");
  }

  // function handlePauseSpeech() {
  //   if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

  //   const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

  //   synth.pause();

  //   console.log("PAUSE REQUEST: ", {
  //     paused: synth.paused,
  //     speaking: synth.speaking,
  //     pending: synth.pending,
  //   })

  //   setTtsStatus("paused");
  // }


  function handlePauseSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    if (readingProgressRef.current.status !== "playing") return;

    // Cancle the current speech session, but preserve reading progress.

    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    readingProgressRef.current.status = "paused";

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("paused");

  }


  async function handleTranslate() {
    if (!extractedText.trim()) {
      alert("Please extract the PDF text first.");
      return;
    }

    if (selectedLanguage === "en") {
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setTranslating(true);
      setTranslationError("");

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(extractedText);

      // const testText =
      //   "At the core of quantum information processing is the quantum bit, or qubit.";

      // const testText = "The sun rises in the east.";

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(testText);

      setTranslationProgress("Preparing translation...");

      const translated = await translateToHindi(
        extractedText,
        (completed, total) => {
          setTranslationProgress(
            `Translating ${completed} of ${total} chunks...`
          );
        }
      );

      setTranslatedText(translated);

      console.log("HINDI TRANSLATION:", translated);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Translation failed:", error);

      setTranslationError(
        error?.message || "Unable to translate this page."
      );

    } finally {
      setTranslating(false);
      setTranslationProgress("");
    }
  }


  if (productLoading) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <p>Loading your book...</p>
      </main>
    )
  }


  if (!product) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <h1>Book not found</h1>
        <Link to="/">Return to SKS Dream</Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="reader-page">
      <header className="reader-header">
        <Link to="/" className="reader-back">
          ← Back to SKS Dream
        </Link>
        <span className="reader-brand">SKS Dream</span>
      </header>

      <section className="reader-heading">
        <p className="reader-eyebrow">DIGITAL LIBRARY</p>
        <h1>{product.name}</h1>
        <p>Settle in, turn the pages, and enjoy your reading experience.</p>
      </section>

      {loading && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="status">
          Preparing your book…
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="alert">
          <p>{error}</p>
          <Link to="/">Return to products</Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* {!loading && !error && pages.length > 0 && ( */}
      {!loading && !error && pdfDocument && totalPages > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* Zooom Effects */}

          <nav
            className="reader-zoom-toolbar"
            aria-label="Reading zoom controls"
          >
            <span className="reader-zoom-label">Reading View</span>

            <div className="reader-zoom-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomOut}
                disabled={zoom <= 75}
                aria-label="Zoom out"
              >
                -
              </button>

              <span className="reader-zoom-level">{zoom}%</span>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomIn}
                disabled={zoom >= 200}
                aria-label="Zoom in"
              >
                +
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="reader-zoom-reset"
                onClick={resetZoom}
              >
                Reset
              </button>
            </div>
          </nav>

          <div className="reader-book-viewport">
            <div className="reader-book-zoom" style={{ zoom: zoom / 100 }}>
              <section className="book-stage" aria-label="Interactive book">
                <HTMLFlipBook
                  ref={flipBookRef}
                  width={380}
                  height={520}
                  size="stretch"
                  minWidth={260}
                  maxWidth={760}
                  minHeight={360}
                  maxHeight={620}
                  showCover
                  usePortrait
                  mobileScrollSupport
                  drawShadow
                  flippingTime={650}
                  maxShadowOpacity={0.45}
                  // onFlip={(event) => {
                  //   // window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                  //   // handleStopSpeech();

                  //   // setCurrentPage(event.data);

                  //   const isAutomaticFlip = autoFlipRef.current;

                  //   autoFlipRef.current = false;

                  //   if (!isAutomaticFlip) {
                  //     handleStopSpeech();
                  //   }

                  //   setCurrentPage(event.data);

                  //   console.log("FLIP SOURCE:", isAutomaticFlip ? "automatic" : "manual");

                  //   const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

                  //   console.log("FLIPBOOK PAGE INFO:", {
                  //     eventPageIndex: event.data,
                  //     currentPageIndex: flipInstance?.getCurrentPageIndex(),
                  //     orientation: flipInstance?.getOrientation(),
                  //     pageCount: flipInstance?.getPageCount(),
                  //   });

                  //   console.log("VISIBLE PDF PAGES:", getVisiblePageNumbers());

                  //   setExtractedText("");
                  //   setTranslatedText("");
                  //   setTranslationError("");
                  // }}

                  onFlip={(event) => {
                    const isAutomaticFlip = autoFlipRef.current;

                    autoFlipRef.current = false;

                    setCurrentPage(event.data);

                    setExtractedText("");
                    setTranslatedText("");
                    setTranslationError("");

                    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

                    console.log(
                      "FLIP SOURCE:",
                      isAutomaticFlip ? "automatic" : "manual"
                    );

                    console.log("VISIBLE PDF PAGES:", visiblePages);

                    if (isAutomaticFlip) {
                      const sessionId = pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current;

                      pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = null;

                      if (
                        sessionId === null ||
                        sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current
                      ) {
                        return;
                      }

                      if (!visiblePages.length) {
                        handleStopSpeech();
                        return;
                      }

                      readingQueueRef.current = [...visiblePages];

                      const firstPage = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

                      readNextVisiblePage(firstPage, sessionId);
                      return;
                    }

                    // Manual navigation.
                    const wasReading = ["playing", "loading", "paused"].includes(
                      readingProgressRef.current.status
                    );

                    handleStopSpeech();

                    // Don't start narration if the reader was already stopped.
                    if (!wasReading || !visiblePages.length) {
                      return;
                    }

                    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

                    readingQueueRef.current = [...visiblePages];

                    const firstPage = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

                    readingProgressRef.current.status = "loading";
                    setTtsStatus("loading");

                    readNextVisiblePage(firstPage, sessionId);
                  }}
                  className="book-flip"
                >
                  {/* {pages.map((page, index) => (
                    <FlipPage key={index} page={page} number={index + 1} />
                  ))} */}

                  {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => (
                    <FlipPage
                      key={`${product.id}-${index}`}
                      pdfDocument={pdfDocument}
                      number={index + 1}
                      shouldRender={Math.abs(index - currentPage) <= 3}
                    />
                  ))}

                </HTMLFlipBook>
              </section>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "20px 0" }} >

            {product?.docxUrl && (
              <button

                onClick={handleReadDocx}
                disabled={docxLoading}
                style={{ marginRight: "10px" }}
              >
                {docxLoading ? "Loading DOCX..." : "Read Clean DOCX Text"}
              </button>
            )}
            <button

              onClick={extractCurrentPageText}
              disabled={extracting}
            >
              {extracting ? "Extracting..." : "Extract Page Text"}
            </button>
            <button

              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Clean Page
            </button>

            <div style={{ marginTop: "15px" }}>

              <label htmlFor="pdf-language">
                Select Language:{" "}
              </label>

              <select id="pdf-language"
                value={selectedLanguage}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setSelectedLanguage(event.target.value)
                  setTranslatedText("");
                  setTranslationError("");
                }}
              >
                <option value="en">English</option>
                <option value="hi">Hindi</option>

              </select>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTranslate}
                disabled={translating || extracting || !extractedText}
                style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
              >
                {translating ? "Translating..." : "Translate"}
              </button>

              {translating && translationProgress && (
                <p
                  style={{
                    marginTop: "10px",
                    fontSize: "14px",
                    color: "#555",
                  }}
                >
                  {translationProgress}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {extractedText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "300px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Extracted PDF Text</h3>

                {/* <p>{extractedText}</p> */}
                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >{
                    selectedLanguage === "hi" && translatedText
                      ? translatedText
                      : extractedText
                  }</p>

                {translationError && (
                  <p
                    style={{ color: "red", marginTop: "10px" }}
                  >{translationError}</p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* DOCX extraction error */}
            {docxError && (
              <p role="alert" style={{ color: "red" }}>
                {docxError}
              </p>
            )}

            {/* Clean DOCX text display */}
            {docxText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "350px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Clean DOCX Text</h3>

                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >
                  {docxText}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}


          </div>

          <div className="reader-controls">

            <select
              value={ttsLanguage}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setTtsLanguage(event.target.value);
                setSelectedVoice("");
              }}
            >
              <option value="en-IN">English</option>
              <option value="hi-IN">Hindi</option>
              <option value="mr-IN">Marathi</option>
              <option value="sa-IN">Sanskrit</option>
            </select>

            <select
              value={selectedVoice}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setSelectedVoice(event.target.value);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">Default Voice</option>

              {ttsVoices
                .filter((voice) =>
                  voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
                    ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()
                  )
                )
                .map((voice) => (
                  <option
                    key={`${voice.name}-${voice.lang}`}
                    value={voice.name}
                  >
                    {voice.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={ttsSpeed}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setTtsSpeed(Number(event.target.value))
              }
            >
              <option value={0.75}>0.75× Slow</option>
              <option value={1}>1× Normal</option>
              <option value={1.25}>1.25× Fast</option>
              <option value={1.5}>1.5× Faster</option>
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSpeakPage}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "loading"}
            >
              {ttsStatus === "paused"
                ? "▶ Resume"
                : ttsStatus === "playing"
                  ? "🔊 Playing"
                  : "🔊 Listen"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handlePauseSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus !== "playing"}
            >
              ⏸ Pause
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStopSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "idle"}
            >
              ⏹ Stop
            </button>

          </div>

          <nav className="reader-controls" aria-label="Book navigation">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goPrevious}
              disabled={currentPage === 0}
            >
              ← Previous
            </button>

            <span>
              {/* Page {currentPage + 1} of {pages.length} */}
              Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goNext}
              // disabled={currentPage >= pages.length - 1}
              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
            >
              Next →
            </button>
          </nav>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

// const FlipPage = forwardRef(function FlipPage({ page, number }, ref) {
//   return (
//     <div className="flip-page" ref={ref}>

const FlipPage = forwardRef(function FlipPage(
  { pdfDocument, number, shouldRender },
  ref,
) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pdfDocument || !shouldRender) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let renderTask;

    async function renderPage() {
      try {
        const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(number);

        if (cancelled) return;

        const canvas = canvasRef.current;
        if (!canvas) return;

        const viewport = pdfPage.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

        canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
        canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

        renderTask = pdfPage.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        });

        await renderTask.promise;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error?.name !== "RenderingCancelledException") {
          console.error("Page rendering failed:", error);
        }
      }
    }

    renderPage();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      renderTask?.cancel();
    };
  }, [pdfDocument, number, shouldRender]);

  return (
    <div className="flip-page" ref={ref}>
      <div className="flip-page-content">
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
      </div>
      <span className="flip-page-number">{number}</span>
    </div>
  );
});

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    let loadingTask;

    if (!product) return;

    async function loadPdf() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      setPages([]);
      setCurrentPage(0);
      setPdfDocument(null);
      setTotalPages(0);
      setExtractedText("");
      setDocxText("");
      pagePreparationCacheRef.current.clear();

      if (!product.pdfUrl) {
        setError("This book does not have a preview available yet.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      try {
        // Public preview: no login and no purchase token required.
        const response = await fetch(product.pdfUrl);

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(
            response.status === 404
              ? "Book preview is not available."
              : "Unable to load book preview.",
          );
        }

        const pdfBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();

        if (cancelled) return;

        loadingTask = pdfjsLib.getDocument({
          data: pdfBuffer,
        });

        const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;

        if (cancelled) {
          await pdf.destroy();
          return;
        }

        setPdfDocument(pdf);
        setTotalPages(pdf.numPages);

        console.log("PUBLIC BOOK PREVIEW LOADED:", {
          productId: product.id,
          previewPages: pdf.numPages,
        });
      } catch (err) {
        if (cancelled) return;

        console.error("Book preview loading failed:", err);

        setError(
          err.message || "We couldn't open this book. Please try again.",
        );
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadPdf();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      loadingTask?.destroy();
    };
  }, [product?.id, product?.pdfUrl]);

  async function extractCurrentPageText() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    try {
      setExtracting(true);
      setExtractedText("");
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");

      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const cleanText = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl: product?.docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
      });

      setExtractedText(cleanText);

      console.log("CLEAN PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("CLEAN TEXT:", cleanText);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Clean text extraction failed:", error);
      alert(error.message || "Unable to extract page text.");
    } finally {
      setExtracting(false);
    }
  }

  function getVisiblePageNumbers() {
    const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

    if (!flipInstance || !totalPages) {
      return [];
    }

    const currentIndex = flipInstance.getCurrentPageIndex();
    const orientation = flipInstance.getOrientation();

    if (currentIndex < 0 || currentIndex >= totalPages) {
      return [];
    }

    const firstPage = currentIndex + 1;

    // Mobile: one page at a time.
    if (orientation === "portrait") {
      return [firstPage];
    }

    // if (secondPage <= totalPages) {
    //   return [firstPage, secondPage];
    // }

    // return [firstPage];

    // Desktop: cover is displayed alon
    if (currentIndex === 0) {
      return [1];
    }

    // Desktop: two-page spread
    const secondPage = firstPage + 1;

    return secondPage <= totalPages ? [firstPage, secondPage] : [firstPage];
  }

  function flipToNextReadingPages() {
    const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

    if (!flipInstance) return false;

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) return false;

    const lastVisiblePage = visiblePages[visiblePages.length - 1];

    if (lastVisiblePage >= totalPages) {
      return false;
    }

    autoFlipRef.current = true;

    flipInstance.flipNext();

    return true;
  }

  function preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber) {
    // if (!pdfDocument || !product?.docxUrl) {
    //   return Promise.reject(new Error("PDF or DOCX is not ready."));
    // }

    if (!pdfDocument || (!product?.docxUrl && !product?.previewTextUrl)) {
      return Promise.reject(new Error("PDF or preview text is not ready."));
    }

    const cache = pagePreparationCacheRef.current;

    // Already prepared or currently preperting

    if (cache.has(pageNumber)) {
      return cache.get(pageNumber);
    }

    console.log("BACKGROUND PREPARATION START:", pageNumber);

    const preparationPromise = getReadablePageText({
      docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
      previewTextUrl: product.previewTextUrl,
      pdfDocument,
      pageNumber,
    })
      .then((text) => {
        if (!text?.trim()) {
          throw new Error(`No readable text found on Page ${pageNumber}`);
        }

        console.log("BACKGROUND PREPARATION READY:", pageNumber);

        return text.trim();
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        //Failed results should not stay cached.

        if (cache.get(pageNumber) === preparationPromise) {
          cache.delete(pageNumber);
        }

        throw error;
      });

    cache.set(pageNumber, preparationPromise);

    return preparationPromise;
  }

  function preloadUpcomingPage(pageNumber, sessionId) {
    if (!pageNumber || pageNumber < 1 || pageNumber > totalPages) {
      return;
    }

    preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber).catch((error) => {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.warn("BACKGROUND PAGE PREPARATION FAILED:", pageNumber, error);
    });
  }

  function completeReadingPage(sessionId) {
    if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

    const nextPageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

    if (nextPageNumber) {
      readNextVisiblePage(nextPageNumber, sessionId);
      return;
    }

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) {
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      return;
    }

    const lastVisiblePage = visiblePages[visiblePages.length - 1];

    if (lastVisiblePage >= totalPages) {
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      console.log("BOOK READING COMPLETED");
      return;
    }

    pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = sessionId;

    readingProgressRef.current.status = "loading";
    setTtsStatus("loading");

    const flipped = flipToNextReadingPages();

    if (!flipped) {
      pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = null;
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
    }
  }

  async function readNextVisiblePage(pageNumber, sessionId) {
    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    try {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      setTtsStatus("loading");

      // const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
      //   docxUrl: product?.docxUrl,
      //   pdfDocument,
      //   pageNumber,
      // });

      console.log("Using PREPARE PAGE:", pageNumber);

      const pageText = await preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber);

      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      if (!pageText?.trim()) {
        throw new Error(`No readable text found on page ${pageNumber}`);
      }

      const chunks = [];
      const maxChunkLength = 200;
      let remainingText = pageText.trim();

      while (remainingText.length > 0) {
        if (remainingText.length <= maxChunkLength) {
          chunks.push(remainingText);
          break;
        }

        let splitAt = remainingText.lastIndexOf(" ", maxChunkLength);

        if (splitAt <= 0) splitAt = maxChunkLength;

        const chunk = remainingText.slice(0, splitAt).trim();

        if (chunk) chunks.push(chunk);

        remainingText = remainingText.slice(splitAt).trim();
      }

      readingProgressRef.current = {
        pageNumber,
        chunks,
        chunkIndex: 0,
        status: "loading",
      };

      const upcomingPage =
        readingQueueRef.current[0] ||
        (pageNumber < totalPages ? pageNumber + 1 : null);

      preloadUpcomingPage(upcomingPage, sessionId);

      // if (upcomingPage) {
      //   preparePageForSpeech(upcomingPage).catch((error) => {
      //     if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      //     console.warn(
      //       "UPCOMING PAGE PREPARATION FAILED:",
      //       upcomingPage,
      //       error
      //     )

      //   })
      // }

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang
              .toLowerCase()
              .startsWith(ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()),
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) => item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase(),
        );

      let chunkIndex = 0;

      const speakNextChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

        readingProgressRef.current.chunkIndex = chunkIndex;

        // if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
        //   console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED:", pageNumber);

        //   const nextPageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

        //   if (nextPageNumber) {
        //     readNextVisiblePage(nextPageNumber, sessionId);
        //   } else {
        //     readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
        //     setTtsStatus("idle");
        //     console.log("VISIBLE PAGES READING COMPLETED");
        //   }

        //   return;
        // }

        if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
          console.log("TTS PAGE COMPLETED:", pageNumber);

          completeReadingPage(sessionId);
          return;
        }

        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(chunks[chunkIndex]);

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

        utterance.onstart = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          readingProgressRef.current.status = "playing";
          setTtsStatus("playing");
        };

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          chunkIndex += 1;
          speakNextChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.error("TTS error:", event.error);
          readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
      speakNextChunk();
    } catch (error) {
      if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

      console.error("Next page reading failed:", error);
      readingQueueRef.current = [];
      readingProgressRef.current.status = "idle";
      setTtsStatus("idle");
    }
  }

  async function handleSpeakPage() {
    if (!pdfDocument) return;

    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      alert("Text-to-Speech is not supported in this browser.");
      return;
    }

    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

    // Resume from paused position.
    // if (synth.paused) {
    //   synth.resume();
    //   setTtsStatus("playing");
    //   return;
    // }

    if (ttsStatus === "paused") {
      // console.log("RESUME REQUEST: ", {
      //   paused: synth.paused,
      //   speaking: synth.speaking,
      //   pending: synth.pending,
      // })

      const progress = readingProgressRef.current;

      if (!progress.chunks.length) {
        setTtsStatus("idle");
        return;
      }

      // if (synth.paused) {
      //   synth.resume();
      //   setTtsStatus("playing");
      // }
      // else {
      //   console.warn("Resume requested, but browser has no paused speech.")
      // }

      const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

      const voice =
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) =>
            item.name === selectedVoice &&
            item.lang
              .toLowerCase()
              .startsWith(ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()),
        ) ||
        ttsVoices.find(
          (item) => item.lang.toLowerCase() === ttsLanguage.toLowerCase(),
        );

      progress.status = "playing";
      setTtsStatus("playing");

      const resumeChunk = () => {
        if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

        // if (progress.chunkIndex >= progress.chunks.length) {
        //   progress.status = "idle";
        //   setTtsStatus("idle");
        //   return;
        // }

        if (progress.chunkIndex >= progress.chunks.length) {
          console.log("RESUMED PAGE COMPLETED:", progress.pageNumber);

          completeReadingPage(sessionId);
          return;
        }

        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
          progress.chunks[progress.chunkIndex],
        );

        utterance.lang = ttsLanguage;
        utterance.rate = ttsSpeed;
        utterance.pitch = 1;
        utterance.volume = 1;

        if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

        utterance.onend = () => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          progress.chunkIndex += 1;
          resumeChunk();
        };

        utterance.onerror = (event) => {
          if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

          console.error("Resume TTS error:", event.error);
          progress.status = "idle";
          setTtsStatus("idle");
        };

        synth.speak(utterance);
      };

      resumeChunk();

      return;
    }
    // Avoid restarting speech when already playing.
    if (synth.speaking || ttsStatus === "loading") {
      return;
    }

    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

    if (!visiblePages.length) {
      setTtsStatus("idle");
      return;
    }

    readingQueueRef.current = [...visiblePages];

    const firstPage = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

    readingProgressRef.current.status = "loading";
    setTtsStatus("loading");

    readNextVisiblePage(firstPage, sessionId);

    // try {
    //   setTtsStatus("loading");

    // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;
    // const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
    // const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

    // console.table(
    //   textContent.items.slice(0, 30).map((item, index) => ({
    //     index,
    //     text: item.str,
    //     fontName: item.fontName,
    //     hasEOL: item.hasEOL,
    //   }))
    // )

    // const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

    // const pageNumber = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

    // const pageText = await getReadablePageText({
    //   docxUrl: product.docxUrl,
    //   pdfDocument,
    //   pageNumber,
    // });

    // if (sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current) return;

    // const pageText = textContent.items
    //   .map((item) => item.str || "")
    //   .join(" ")
    //   .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    //   .trim();

    //   if (!pageText) {
    //     setTtsStatus("idle");
    //     alert("No readable text found on this page.");
    //     return;

    //   console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageNumber);
