  // function handlePauseSpeech() {
  //   if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

  //   window.speechSynthesis.pause();
  //   setTtsStatus("paused");
  // }

  function handleStopSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    // Invalidate pending and future chunks.
    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = null;
    autoFlipRef.current = false;

    readingQueueRef.current = [];

    readingProgressRef.current = {
      pageNumber: null,
      chunks: [],
      chunkIndex: 0,
      status: "idle",
    };

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("idle");
  }

  // function handlePauseSpeech() {
  //   if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

  //   const synth = window.speechSynthesis;

  //   synth.pause();

  //   console.log("PAUSE REQUEST: ", {
  //     paused: synth.paused,
  //     speaking: synth.speaking,
  //     pending: synth.pending,
  //   })

  //   setTtsStatus("paused");
  // }

  function handlePauseSpeech() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

    if (readingProgressRef.current.status !== "playing") return;

    // Cancle the current speech session, but preserve reading progress.

    speechSessionRef.current += 1;

    readingProgressRef.current.status = "paused";

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    setTtsStatus("paused");
  }

  async function handleTranslate() {
    if (!extractedText.trim()) {
      alert("Please extract the PDF text first.");
      return;
    }

    if (selectedLanguage === "en") {
      setTranslatedText("");
      setTranslationError("");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setTranslating(true);
      setTranslationError("");

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(extractedText);

      // const testText =
      //   "At the core of quantum information processing is the quantum bit, or qubit.";

      // const testText = "The sun rises in the east.";

      // const translated = await translateToHindi(testText);

      setTranslationProgress("Preparing translation...");

      const translated = await translateToHindi(
        extractedText,
        (completed, total) => {
          setTranslationProgress(
            `Translating ${completed} of ${total} chunks...`,
          );
        },
      );

      setTranslatedText(translated);

      console.log("HINDI TRANSLATION:", translated);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Translation failed:", error);

      setTranslationError(error?.message || "Unable to translate this page.");
    } finally {
      setTranslating(false);
      setTranslationProgress("");
    }
  }

  if (productLoading) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <p>Loading your book...</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (!product) {
    return (
      <main className="reader-message">
        <h1>Book not found</h1>
        <Link to="/">Return to SKS Dream</Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="reader-page">
      <header className="reader-header">
        <Link to="/" className="reader-back">
          ← Back to SKS Dream
        </Link>
        <span className="reader-brand">SKS Dream</span>
      </header>

      <section className="reader-heading">
        <p className="reader-eyebrow">DIGITAL LIBRARY</p>
        <h1>{product.name}</h1>
        <p>Settle in, turn the pages, and enjoy your reading experience.</p>
      </section>

      {loading && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="status">
          Preparing your book…
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="reader-message" role="alert">
          <p>{error}</p>
          <Link to="/">Return to products</Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* {!loading && !error && pages.length > 0 && ( */}
      {!loading && !error && pdfDocument && totalPages > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* Zooom Effects */}

          <nav
            className="reader-zoom-toolbar"
            aria-label="Reading zoom controls"
          >
            <span className="reader-zoom-label">Reading View</span>

            <div className="reader-zoom-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomOut}
                disabled={zoom <= 75}
                aria-label="Zoom out"
              >
                -
              </button>

              <span className="reader-zoom-level">{zoom}%</span>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={zoomIn}
                disabled={zoom >= 200}
                aria-label="Zoom in"
              >
                +
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="reader-zoom-reset"
                onClick={resetZoom}
              >
                Reset
              </button>
            </div>
          </nav>

          <div className="reader-book-viewport">
            <div className="reader-book-zoom" style={{ zoom: zoom / 100 }}>
              <section className="book-stage" aria-label="Interactive book">
                <HTMLFlipBook
                  ref={flipBookRef}
                  width={380}
                  height={520}
                  size="stretch"
                  minWidth={260}
                  maxWidth={760}
                  minHeight={360}
                  maxHeight={620}
                  showCover
                  usePortrait
                  mobileScrollSupport
                  drawShadow
                  flippingTime={650}
                  maxShadowOpacity={0.45}
                  // onFlip={(event) => {
                  //   // window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                  //   // handleStopSpeech();

                  //   // setCurrentPage(event.data);

                  //   const isAutomaticFlip = autoFlipRef.current;

                  //   autoFlipRef.current = false;

                  //   if (!isAutomaticFlip) {
                  //     handleStopSpeech();
                  //   }

                  //   setCurrentPage(event.data);

                  //   console.log("FLIP SOURCE:", isAutomaticFlip ? "automatic" : "manual");

                  //   const flipInstance = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

                  //   console.log("FLIPBOOK PAGE INFO:", {
                  //     eventPageIndex: event.data,
                  //     currentPageIndex: flipInstance?.getCurrentPageIndex(),
                  //     orientation: flipInstance?.getOrientation(),
                  //     pageCount: flipInstance?.getPageCount(),
                  //   });

                  //   console.log("VISIBLE PDF PAGES:", getVisiblePageNumbers());

                  //   setExtractedText("");
                  //   setTranslatedText("");
                  //   setTranslationError("");
                  // }}

                  onFlip={(event) => {
                    const isAutomaticFlip = autoFlipRef.current;

                    autoFlipRef.current = false;

                    setCurrentPage(event.data);

                    setExtractedText("");
                    setTranslatedText("");
                    setTranslationError("");

                    const visiblePages = getVisiblePageNumbers();

                    console.log(
                      "FLIP SOURCE:",
                      isAutomaticFlip ? "automatic" : "manual",
                    );

                    console.log("VISIBLE PDF PAGES:", visiblePages);

                    if (isAutomaticFlip) {
                      const sessionId = pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current;

                      pendingAutoFlipSessionRef.current = null;

                      if (
                        sessionId === null ||
                        sessionId !== speechSessionRef.current
                      ) {
                        return;
                      }

                      if (!visiblePages.length) {
                        handleStopSpeech();
                        return;
                      }

                      readingQueueRef.current = [...visiblePages];

                      const firstPage = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

                      readNextVisiblePage(firstPage, sessionId);
                      return;
                    }

                    // Manual navigation.
                    const wasReading = [
                      "playing",
                      "loading",
                      "paused",
                    ].includes(readingProgressRef.current.status);

                    handleStopSpeech();

                    // Don't start narration if the reader was already stopped.
                    if (!wasReading || !visiblePages.length) {
                      return;
                    }

                    const sessionId = ++speechSessionRef.current;

                    readingQueueRef.current = [...visiblePages];

                    const firstPage = readingQueueRef.current.shift();

                    readingProgressRef.current.status = "loading";
                    setTtsStatus("loading");

                    readNextVisiblePage(firstPage, sessionId);
                  }}
                  className="book-flip"
                >
                  {/* {pages.map((page, index) => (
                    <FlipPage key={index} page={page} number={index + 1} />
                  ))} */}

                  {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => (
                    <FlipPage
                      key={`${product.id}-${index}`}
                      pdfDocument={pdfDocument}
                      number={index + 1}
                      shouldRender={Math.abs(index - currentPage) <= 3}
                    />
                  ))}
                </HTMLFlipBook>
              </section>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "20px 0" }}>
            {product?.docxUrl && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleReadDocx}
                disabled={docxLoading}
                style={{ marginRight: "10px" }}
              >
                {docxLoading ? "Loading DOCX..." : "Read Clean DOCX Text"}
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={extractCurrentPageText}
              disabled={extracting}
            >
              {extracting ? "Extracting..." : "Extract Page Text"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestOCR}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Marathi OCR
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestDocxMatch}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              TEST DOCX Matching
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestPageBoundaries}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Page Boundaries
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestCleanPage}
              style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
            >
              Test Clean Page
            </button>

            <div style={{ marginTop: "15px" }}>
              <label htmlFor="pdf-language">Select Language: </label>

              <select
                id="pdf-language"
                value={selectedLanguage}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setSelectedLanguage(event.target.value);
                  setTranslatedText("");
                  setTranslationError("");
                }}
              >
                <option value="en">English</option>
                <option value="hi">Hindi</option>
              </select>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTranslate}
                disabled={translating || extracting || !extractedText}
                style={{ marginLeft: "10px" }}
              >
                {translating ? "Translating..." : "Translate"}
              </button>

              {translating && translationProgress && (
                <p
                  style={{
                    marginTop: "10px",
                    fontSize: "14px",
                    color: "#555",
                  }}
                >
                  {translationProgress}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {extractedText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "300px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Extracted PDF Text</h3>

                {/* <p>{extractedText}</p> */}
                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >
                  {selectedLanguage === "hi" && translatedText
                    ? translatedText
                    : extractedText}
                </p>

                {translationError && (
                  <p style={{ color: "red", marginTop: "10px" }}>
                    {translationError}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* DOCX extraction error */}
            {docxError && (
              <p role="alert" style={{ color: "red" }}>
                {docxError}
              </p>
            )}

            {/* Clean DOCX text display */}
            {docxText && (
              <div
                style={{
                  marginTop: "20px",
                  padding: "20px",
                  background: "#f5f5f5",
                  borderRadius: "10px",
                  textAlign: "left",
                  maxHeight: "350px",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                }}
              >
                <h3>Clean DOCX Text</h3>

                <p
                  style={{
                    whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                    lineHeight: "1.8",
                  }}
                >
                  {docxText}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="reader-controls">
            <select
              value={ttsLanguage}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setTtsLanguage(event.target.value);
                setSelectedVoice("");
              }}
            >
              <option value="en-IN">English</option>
              <option value="hi-IN">Hindi</option>
              <option value="mr-IN">Marathi</option>
              <option value="sa-IN">Sanskrit</option>
            </select>

            <select
              value={selectedVoice}
              onChange={(event) => {
                handleStopSpeech();
                setSelectedVoice(event.target.value);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">Default Voice</option>

              {ttsVoices
                .filter((voice) =>
                  voice.lang
                    .toLowerCase()
                    .startsWith(ttsLanguage.split("-")[0].toLowerCase()),
                )
                .map((voice) => (
                  <option
                    key={`${voice.name}-${voice.lang}`}
                    value={voice.name}
                  >
                    {voice.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={ttsSpeed}
              onChange={(event) => setTtsSpeed(Number(event.target.value))}
            >
              <option value={0.75}>0.75× Slow</option>
              <option value={1}>1× Normal</option>
              <option value={1.25}>1.25× Fast</option>
              <option value={1.5}>1.5× Faster</option>
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSpeakPage}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "loading"}
            >
              {ttsStatus === "paused"
                ? "▶ Resume"
                : ttsStatus === "playing"
                  ? "🔊 Playing"
                  : "🔊 Listen"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handlePauseSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus !== "playing"}
            >
              ⏸ Pause
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStopSpeech}
              disabled={ttsStatus === "idle"}
            >
              ⏹ Stop
            </button>
          </div>

          <nav className="reader-controls" aria-label="Book navigation">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goPrevious}
              disabled={currentPage === 0}
            >
              ← Previous
            </button>

            <span>
              {/* Page {currentPage + 1} of {pages.length} */}
              Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={goNext}
              // disabled={currentPage >= pages.length - 1}
              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
            >
              Next →
            </button>
          </nav>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

import React, {
  forwardRef,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import axios from "axios";
import HTMLFlipBook from "react-pageflip";
import * as pdfjsLib from "pdfjs-dist";
import pdfWorker from "pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.min.mjs?url";
import "../../BookReader.css";
import Chant from "./Chant"

import {
  prepareMixedSpeechChunks,
} from "../../Services/MixedBookTextService";

import { getReadablePageText } from "../../Services/BookTextService";


pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = pdfWorker;

// const API_URL = "http://localhost:4000" || "https://sks-serverproject-new.onrender.com";
const API_URL = "https://sks-serverproject-new.onrender.com";

const PurchasedPage = forwardRef(function PurchasedPage(
  { pdfDocument, number, shouldRender, zoom },
  ref
) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const containerRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pdfDocument || !shouldRender) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let renderTask = null;
    let frameId = null;
    let resizeObserver = null;
    let renderVersion = 0;

    async function renderPage() {
      const version = ++renderVersion;

      if (renderTask) {
        renderTask.cancel();

        try {
          await renderTask.promise;
        } catch {
          // Previous rendering was cancelled.
        }

        renderTask = null;
      }

      if (cancelled || version !== renderVersion) return;

      try {
        const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(number);

        if (cancelled || version !== renderVersion) return;

        const canvas = canvasRef.current;
        const container = containerRef.current;

        if (!canvas || !container) return;

        const displayWidth =
          container.offsetWidth * (zoom / 100) * 1.5;

        if (displayWidth <= 0) return;

        const baseViewport = pdfPage.getViewport({ scale: 1 });

        const dpr = Math.min(
          Math.max(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2),
          2.5
        );

        const targetScale =
          (displayWidth / baseViewport.width) * dpr;

        const maxPixels = 12000000;
        const maxDimension = 4096;

        const scaleLimit = Math.min(
          maxDimension / baseViewport.width,
          maxDimension / baseViewport.height,
          Math.sqrt(
            maxPixels /
            (baseViewport.width * baseViewport.height)
          )
        );

        const renderScale = Math.min(
          targetScale,
          scaleLimit
        );

        const viewport = pdfPage.getViewport({
          scale: renderScale,
        });

        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
        if (!context) return;

        canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
        canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

        if (cancelled || version !== renderVersion) return;

        renderTask = pdfPage.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        });

        await renderTask.promise;
      } catch (error) {
        if (
          !cancelled &&
          version === renderVersion &&
          error?.name !== "RenderingCancelledException"
        ) {
          console.error("Purchased PDF rendering failed:", error);
        }
      }
    }

    function scheduleRender() {
      if (frameId !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
      }

      frameId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        frameId = null;
        renderPage();
      });
    }

    const container = containerRef.current;

    if (container) {
      resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(scheduleRender);
      resizeObserver.observe(container);
    }

    scheduleRender();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      renderVersion += 1;

      if (frameId !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
      }

      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
      renderTask?.cancel();
    };
  }, [pdfDocument, number, shouldRender, zoom]);

  return (
    <div className="flip-page" ref={ref}>
      <div className="flip-page-content" ref={containerRef}>
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
      </div>
      <span className="flip-page-number">{number}</span>
    </div>
  );
});


export default function PurchasedBookReader() {
  const { productId } = useParams();

  const isMixedLanguageBook = Number(productId) === 9;

  const flipBookRef = useRef(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [bookTitle, setBookTitle] = useState("");
  const [pdfDocument, setPdfDocument] = useState(null);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(0);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(100);


  const [speakingStatus, setSpeakingStatus] = useState("idle");
  const [speakingError, setSpeakingError] = useState("");
  const [speechRate, setSpeechRate] = useState(0.9);
  const [speechVoices, setSpeechVoices] = useState([]);
  const [selectedVoice, setSelectedVoice] = useState("");

  const speechSessionRef = useRef(0);
  const speechPausedRef = useRef(false);
  const speechTimeoutRef = useRef(null);
  const speechUtteranceRef = useRef(null);
  const speechStatusRef = useRef("idle");

  const autoFlipTargetRef = useRef(null);

  // 

  const autoFlipTimeoutRef = useRef(null);
  const autoFlipRetryRef = useRef(0);

  const pagePreparationCacheRef = useRef(new Map());
  const pendingSpeechContinuationRef = useRef(null);

  const autoFlipContinuationRef = useRef(null);

  const [docxUrl, setDocxUrl] = useState("");

  const [isChantProduct, setIsChantProduct] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    let loadingTask;
    let objectUrl;
    let docxObjectUrl;

    async function loadPurchasedBook() {
      pagePreparationCacheRef.current.clear();

      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      setBookTitle("");
      setPdfDocument(null);
      setTotalPages(0);
      setCurrentPage(0);
      setDocxUrl("");
      setIsChantProduct(false);
      pagePreparationCacheRef.current.clear();

      try {
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem("authToken");

        if (!token) {
          throw new Error("Please log in to read your book.");
        }

        const headers = {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        };

        const accessResponse = await axios.get(
          `${API_URL}/api/library/${productId}/access`,
          { headers, signal: controller.signal }
        );

        if (accessResponse.data?.data?.hasAccess !== true) {
          throw new Error("Purchase required to read this book.");
        }

        // Get the purchased book title.
        const booksResponse = await axios.get(
          `${API_URL}/api/library/my-books`,
          { headers, signal: controller.signal }
        );

        const purchasedBook = booksResponse.data?.data?.find(
          (book) => Number(book.product_id) === Number(productId)
        );

        setBookTitle(purchasedBook?.title || "My Purchased Book");

        if (Number(productId) === 1) {
          setIsChantProduct(true);
          return;
        }

        // Download the protected PDF with authentication.
        const pdfResponse = await axios.get(
          `${API_URL}/api/library/${productId}/pdf`,
          {
            headers,
            responseType: "blob",
            signal: controller.signal,
          }
        );

        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;

        objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(pdfResponse.data);

        loadingTask = pdfjsLib.getDocument({
          url: objectUrl,
        });

        const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;

        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;

        setPdfDocument(pdf);
        setTotalPages(pdf.numPages);

        const docxResponse = await axios.get(
          `${API_URL}/api/library/${productId}/docx`,
          {
            headers,
            responseType: "blob",
            signal: controller.signal,
          }
        );

        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;

        const purchasedDocxUrl = URL.createObjectURL(docxResponse.data);
        docxObjectUrl = purchasedDocxUrl;
        setDocxUrl(purchasedDocxUrl);


      } catch (err) {
        if (
          controller.signal.aborted ||
          err.code === "ERR_CANCELED"
        ) {
          return;
        }

        console.error("Purchased book loading failed:", err);

        if (err.response?.status === 404) {
          setError("This book's PDF has not been uploaded yet.");
        } else if (err.response?.status === 401) {
          setError("Your session has expired. Please log in again.");
        } else if (err.response?.status === 403) {
          setError("You do not have access to this book.");
        } else {
          setError(err.message || "Unable to load purchased book.");
        }
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadPurchasedBook();

    return () => {

      speechSessionRef.current += 1;

      if (speechTimeoutRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(speechTimeoutRef.current);
      }

      if ("speechSynthesis" in window) {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      }

      controller.abort();
      loadingTask?.destroy();
      if (objectUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
      if (docxObjectUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(docxObjectUrl);
    };
  }, [productId]);

  async function testCurrentPageText() {
    try {
      const pageNumber = currentPage + 1;

      const text = await getReadablePageText({
        docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
        language: isMixedLanguageBook ? "mixed" : undefined,

      });

      console.log("PURCHASED BOOK PAGE:", pageNumber);
      console.log("MATCHED DOCX TEXT:", text);
      alert(`Page ${pageNumber}: ${text.length} characters matched.`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Purchased page matching failed:", error);
      alert(error.message);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      setSpeakingError("Browser speech is not supported.");
      return;
    }

    function loadVoices() {
      const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();

      setSpeechVoices(voices);

      setSelectedVoice((previous) => {
        if (previous) return previous;

        const preferred =
          voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase() === "mr-in") ||
          voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith("mr")) ||
          voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase() === "en-in") ||
          voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith("en")) ||
          voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith("hi")) ||
          voices[0];

        return preferred?.voiceURI || "";
      });
    }

    loadVoices();
    window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener("voiceschanged", loadVoices);

    return () => {
      window.speechSynthesis.removeEventListener("voiceschanged", loadVoices);
    };
  }, []);



  function stopSpeaking() {
    speechSessionRef.current += 1;
    autoFlipTargetRef.current = null;
    pendingSpeechContinuationRef.current = null;
    autoFlipContinuationRef.current = null;
    speechPausedRef.current = false;
    speechStatusRef.current = "idle";



    if (speechTimeoutRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(speechTimeoutRef.current);
      speechTimeoutRef.current = null;
    }

    if (autoFlipTimeoutRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(autoFlipTimeoutRef.current);
      autoFlipTimeoutRef.current = null;
    }

    autoFlipRetryRef.current = 0;

    speechUtteranceRef.current = null;

    if ("speechSynthesis" in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    }

    setSpeakingStatus("idle");
  }

  function pauseSpeaking() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;
    if (speechStatusRef.current !== "speaking") return;

    speechPausedRef.current = true;
    speechStatusRef.current = "paused";

    window.speechSynthesis.pause();
    setSpeakingStatus("paused");
  }

  function resumeSpeaking() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;
    if (speechStatusRef.current !== "paused") return;

    speechPausedRef.current = false;
    speechStatusRef.current = "speaking";

    window.speechSynthesis.resume();
    setSpeakingStatus("speaking");

    const continuation = pendingSpeechContinuationRef.current;

    if (typeof continuation === "function") {
      pendingSpeechContinuationRef.current = null;
      continuation();
    }
  }

  // function prepareSpeechChunks(text) {
  //   const normalized = String(text || "")
  //     .replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
  //     .replace(/[ \t]+/g, " ")
  //     .trim();

  //   const paragraphs = normalized
  //     .split(/\n\s*\n/)
  //     .filter(Boolean);

  //   const chunks = [];

  //   for (const paragraph of paragraphs) {
  //     const sentences = paragraph
  //       .split(/(?<=[।॥.!?])\s+/u)
  //       .filter(Boolean);

  //     for (const sentence of sentences) {
  //       // Keep utterances reasonably short for browser TTS.
  //       const words = sentence.split(/\s+/);
  //       let buffer = "";

  //       for (const word of words) {
  //         if (buffer && (buffer + " " + word).length > 180) {
  //           chunks.push({
  //             text: buffer,
  //             // pauseMs: 180,
  //             pauseMs: 80,
  //           });
  //           buffer = word;
  //         } else {
  //           buffer = buffer ? `${buffer} ${word}` : word;
  //         }
  //       }

  //       if (buffer) {
  //         chunks.push({
  //           text: buffer,
  //           // pauseMs: 350,
  //           pauseMs: 150,
  //         });
  //       }
  //     }

  //     if (chunks.length) {
  //       // chunks[chunks.length - 1].pauseMs = 650;
  //       chunks[chunks.length - 1].pauseMs = 250;
  //     }
  //   }

  //   return chunks;
  // }



  function prepareSpeechChunks(text) {
    const normalized = String(text || "")
      .normalize("NFC")
      .replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n")
      .replace(/[ \t]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/([^\n])\n(?!\n)/g, "$1 ")
      .replace(/\n{2,}/g, "\n\n")
      .trim();

    const paragraphs = normalized
      .split(/\n\s*\n/)
      .map((paragraph) => paragraph.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    const chunks = [];

    for (const paragraph of paragraphs) {
      const sentences = paragraph
        .split(/(?<=[।॥.!?])\s+/u)
        .map((sentence) => sentence.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);

      for (const sentence of sentences) {
        const words = sentence.split(/\s+/);
        let buffer = "";

        for (const word of words) {
          if (buffer && (buffer + " " + word).length > 240) {
            chunks.push({
              text: buffer,
              pauseMs: 0,
            });
            buffer = word;
          } else {
            buffer = buffer ? `${buffer} ${word}` : word;
          }
        }

        if (buffer) {
          chunks.push({
            text: buffer,
            pauseMs: 80,
          });
        }
      }

      if (chunks.length) {
        chunks[chunks.length - 1].pauseMs = 150;
      }
    }

    return chunks;
  }

  // const selectedSpeechVoice = speechVoices.find(
  //   (item) => item.voiceURI === selectedVoice
  // );

  // const selectedOCRLanguage = selectedSpeechVoice?.lang || "mr-IN";

  const selectedSpeechVoice = speechVoices.find(
    (item) => item.voiceURI === selectedVoice
  );

  // OCR language is based on the book, not the selected TTS voice.
  // const selectedOCRLanguage = "mr-IN";


  // function preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber) {
  //   if (!docxUrl || !pdfDocument) {
  //     return Promise.reject(new Error("PDF or DOCX is not ready."));
  //   }

  //   const cache = pagePreparationCacheRef.current;

  //   if (cache.has(pageNumber)) {
  //     return cache.get(pageNumber);
  //   }

  //   const promise = getReadablePageText({
  //     docxUrl,
  //     pdfDocument,
  //     pageNumber,
  //     language: selectedOCRLanguage,
  //   })
  //     .then((text) => {
  //       if (!text?.trim()) {
  //         throw new Error(`Page ${pageNumber}: no verified text.`);
  //       }
  //       return text;
  //     })
  //     .catch((error) => {
  //       if (cache.get(pageNumber) === promise) {
  //         cache.delete(pageNumber);
  //       }
  //       throw error;
  //     });

  //   cache.set(pageNumber, promise);
  //   return promise;
  // }


  function preparePageForSpeech(pageNumber) {
    if (!docxUrl || !pdfDocument) {
      return Promise.reject(new Error("PDF or DOCX is not ready."));
    }

    // const language = selectedOCRLanguage;
    const cache = pagePreparationCacheRef.current;

    // const cacheKey = `${pageNumber}:${language}`;
    // const cacheKey = String(pageNumber);
    const cacheKey = `${docxUrl}:${pageNumber}`;

    if (cache.has(cacheKey)) {
      return cache.get(cacheKey);
    }

    const promise = getReadablePageText({
      docxUrl,
      pdfDocument,
      pageNumber,
      language: isMixedLanguageBook ? "mixed" : undefined,

    })
      .then((text) => {
        if (!text?.trim()) {
          throw new Error(`Page ${pageNumber}: no verified text.`);
        }

        console.log(
          "PAGE OCR:",
          "Language automatically detected by BookTextService"
        );

        console.log(
          "PAGE EXTRACTED TEXT:",
          text.slice(0, 500)
        );

        return text;
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        if (cache.get(cacheKey) === promise) {
          cache.delete(cacheKey);
        }

        throw error;
      });

    cache.set(cacheKey, promise);

    return promise;
  }




  async function startSpeaking() {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
      setSpeakingError("Browser speech is not supported.");
      return;
    }

    if (!docxUrl || !pdfDocument) return;

    stopSpeaking();
    setSpeakingError("");

    const sessionId = speechSessionRef.current;
    const synth = window.speechSynthesis;
    const flip = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

    const initialPage = flip?.getCurrentPageIndex() ?? currentPage;

    const voice = speechVoices.find(
      (item) => item.voiceURI === selectedVoice
    );

    function active() {
      return sessionId === speechSessionRef.current;
    }

    function fail(error, pageIndex) {
      if (!active()) return;
      stopSpeaking();
      setSpeakingError(
        `Page ${pageIndex + 1}: ${error.message || error}`
      );
      console.error("Continuous reading failed:", error);
    }

    function waitWhilePaused(next) {
      if (!active()) return;

      if (speechPausedRef.current) {
        pendingSpeechContinuationRef.current = next;
        speechStatusRef.current = "paused";
        setSpeakingStatus("paused");
        return;
      }

      pendingSpeechContinuationRef.current = null;
      next();
    }

    function scheduleNext(next, delay) {
      if (!active()) return;

      speechTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        speechTimeoutRef.current = null;
        waitWhilePaused(next);
      }, delay);
    }

    async function readPage(pageIndex) {
      if (!active()) return;

      speechStatusRef.current = "loading";
      setSpeakingStatus("loading");

      try {
        const text = await preparePageForSpeech(pageIndex + 1);
        console.log("TTS PAGE:", pageIndex + 1);
        console.log("TTS TEXT START:", text.slice(0, 500));

        if (!active()) return;

        // const chunks = prepareSpeechChunks(text);

        const chunks = isMixedLanguageBook
          ? prepareMixedSpeechChunks(text)
          : prepareSpeechChunks(text);

        if (!chunks.length) {
          throw new Error("No verified speaking text found.");
        }

        // Prepare next page while current page is speaking.
        if (pageIndex + 1 < totalPages) {
          preparePageForSpeech(pageIndex + 2).catch((error) => {
            if (active()) {
              console.warn(
                "Next page preparation failed:",
                pageIndex + 2,
                error
              );
            }
          });
        }

        let chunkIndex = 0;

        function speakNext() {
          if (!active()) return;

          waitWhilePaused(() => {
            if (!active()) return;

            if (chunkIndex >= chunks.length) {
              advancePage(pageIndex);
              return;
            }

            const chunk = chunks[chunkIndex];
            // const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(chunk.text);

            // if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

            // utterance.lang = voice?.lang || "mr-IN";

            const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(chunk.text);

            if (isMixedLanguageBook) {
              const targetVoice = speechVoices.find((item) =>
                item.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(
                  chunk.language === "hi-IN" ? "hi" : "en"
                )
              );

              if (targetVoice) {
                utterance.voice = targetVoice;
              }

              utterance.lang = chunk.language;
            } else {
              if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;

              utterance.lang = voice?.lang || "mr-IN";
            }

            utterance.rate = speechRate;
            utterance.pitch = 1;
            utterance.volume = 1;

            speechUtteranceRef.current = utterance;

            utterance.onstart = () => {
              if (!active()) return;
              if (speechPausedRef.current) {
                synth.pause();
                return;
              }
              speechStatusRef.current = "speaking";
              setSpeakingStatus("speaking");
            };

            utterance.onend = () => {
              if (!active()) return;

              chunkIndex += 1;
              speechUtteranceRef.current = null;

              scheduleNext(speakNext, chunk.pauseMs);
            };

            utterance.onerror = (event) => {
              if (!active()) return;

              if (
                event.error === "canceled" ||
                event.error === "interrupted"
              ) return;

              fail(new Error(`Speech failed: ${event.error}`), pageIndex);
            };

            synth.speak(utterance);
          });
        }

        waitWhilePaused(speakNext);
      } catch (error) {
        fail(error, pageIndex);
      }
    }


    function advancePage(pageIndex) {
      if (!active()) return;

      const nextIndex = pageIndex + 1;

      if (nextIndex >= totalPages) {
        speechStatusRef.current = "idle";
        setSpeakingStatus("idle");
        return;
      }

      waitWhilePaused(() => {
        if (!active()) return;

        const flip = flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip();

        if (!flip) {
          fail(new Error("Flipbook unavailable."), pageIndex);
          return;
        }

        const visiblePageIndices = flip.getPageCount
          ? (() => {
            const current = flip.getCurrentPageIndex();
            const isPortrait =
              flip.getSettings?.()?.usePortrait &&
              flip.getOrientation?.() === "portrait";

            if (isPortrait) return [current];

            // Desktop spread: cover occupies its own spread.
            if (current === 0) return [0];

            const spreadStart = current % 2 === 1
              ? current
              : current - 1;

            return [spreadStart, spreadStart + 1];
          })()
          : [pageIndex];

        // Next page already visible: read without flipping.
        if (visiblePageIndices.includes(nextIndex)) {
          readPage(nextIndex);
          return;
        }

        // Otherwise turn the physical book page.
        // const currentIndex = flip.getCurrentPageIndex();

        // // Target is the next visible spread's first page.
        // const expectedIndex =
        //   currentIndex === 0
        //     ? 1
        //     : currentIndex % 2 === 0
        //       ? currentIndex + 1
        //       : currentIndex + 2;

        // autoFlipTargetRef.current = expectedIndex;

        // autoFlipContinuationRef.current = () => {
        //   if (!active()) return;

        //   autoFlipContinuationRef.current = null;
        //   scheduleNext(() => readPage(nextIndex), 100);
        // };



        // flip.flipNext();

        // speechTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        //   if (!active()) return;

        //   if (autoFlipTargetRef.current !== null) {
        //     fail(
        //       new Error("Automatic page flip did not complete."),
        //       pageIndex
        //     );
        //   }
        // }, 2500);





        // Otherwise turn the physical book page.

        const getExpectedFlipIndex = () => {
          const currentIndex = flip.getCurrentPageIndex();

          const isPortrait =
            flip.getSettings?.()?.usePortrait &&
            flip.getOrientation?.() === "portrait";

          // Portrait: advance one page.
          if (isPortrait) {
            return currentIndex + 1;
          }

          // Landscape: advance to the next visible spread.
          if (currentIndex === 0) {
            return 1;
          }

          return currentIndex % 2 === 0
            ? currentIndex + 1
            : currentIndex + 2;
        };

        autoFlipTargetRef.current = getExpectedFlipIndex();
        autoFlipRetryRef.current = 0;

        autoFlipContinuationRef.current = () => {
          if (!active()) return;

          if (autoFlipTimeoutRef.current) {
            clearTimeout(autoFlipTimeoutRef.current);
            autoFlipTimeoutRef.current = null;
          }

          autoFlipTargetRef.current = null;
          autoFlipContinuationRef.current = null;
          autoFlipRetryRef.current = 0;

          scheduleNext(() => readPage(nextIndex), 150);
        };

        const attemptAutomaticFlip = () => {
          if (!active()) return;

          if (autoFlipTimeoutRef.current) {
            clearTimeout(autoFlipTimeoutRef.current);
            autoFlipTimeoutRef.current = null;
          }

          const targetIndex = autoFlipTargetRef.current;

          if (targetIndex === null) return;

          flip.flipNext();

          autoFlipTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            autoFlipTimeoutRef.current = null;

            if (!active()) return;

            const actualIndex = flip.getCurrentPageIndex();

            // Flip succeeded, even if the onFlip event was missed.
            if (actualIndex === targetIndex) {
              autoFlipContinuationRef.current?.();
              return;
            }

            // Retry only once.
            if (autoFlipRetryRef.current < 1) {
              autoFlipRetryRef.current += 1;

              console.warn(
                "Automatic page flip failed. Retrying...",
                {
                  expected: targetIndex,
                  actual: actualIndex,
                  retry: autoFlipRetryRef.current,
                }
              );

              autoFlipTargetRef.current = getExpectedFlipIndex();

              attemptAutomaticFlip();
              return;
            }

            // Do not terminate continuous reading.
            console.warn(
              "Automatic page flip failed after retry. Continuing speech.",
              {
                expected: autoFlipTargetRef.current,
                actual: actualIndex,
              }
            );

            autoFlipTargetRef.current = null;
            autoFlipContinuationRef.current = null;
            autoFlipRetryRef.current = 0;

            scheduleNext(() => readPage(nextIndex), 300);

          }, 2500);
        };

        attemptAutomaticFlip();


      });
    }


    // Used by the onFlip handler to continue the same speech session.
    // pendingSpeechContinuationRef.current = {
    //   onAutoFlip(nextIndex) {
    //     if (!active()) return;

    //     if (speechTimeoutRef.current) {
    //       clearTimeout(speechTimeoutRef.current);
    //       speechTimeoutRef.current = null;
    //     }

    //     scheduleNext(() => readPage(nextIndex), 100);
    //   },
    // };

    readPage(initialPage);
  }




  function goPrevious() {
    flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip()?.flipPrev();
  }

  function goNext() {
    flipBookRef.current?.pageFlip()?.flipNext();
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="reader-message">
        Preparing your purchased book...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isChantProduct) {
    return <Chant />
  }

  if (error || !pdfDocument) {
    return (
      <div className="reader-message" role="alert">
        {error || "Unable to open this book."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    // <main className="reader-page purchased-reader-page">
    <main className={`reader-page purchased-reader-page ${zoom > 100 ? "zoomed-reading" : ""
      }`}>
      <section className="reader-heading">
        <p className="reader-eyebrow">MY DIGITAL LIBRARY</p>
        <h1>{bookTitle}</h1>
        <p>Enjoy reading your purchased eBook.</p>
      </section>

      <nav className="reader-zoom-toolbar">
        <span className="reader-zoom-label">Reading View</span>

        <div className="reader-zoom-actions">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(75, z - 25))}
            disabled={zoom <= 75}
          >
            -
          </button>

          <span className="reader-zoom-level">{zoom}%</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(200, z + 25))}
            disabled={zoom >= 200}
          >
            +
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="reader-zoom-reset"
            onClick={() => setZoom(100)}
          >
            Reset
          </button>
        </div>
      </nav>



      <section className="reader-zoom-toolbar" aria-label="Speaking controls">
        <span className="reader-zoom-label">
          Read Aloud — Page {currentPage + 1}
        </span>

        <div className="reader-zoom-actions" style={{ flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={startSpeaking}
            disabled={!docxUrl || speakingStatus === "loading"}
            style={{ fontSize: 13 }}
          >
            {speakingStatus === "loading" ? "Matching text..." : "🔊 Read Aloud"}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={pauseSpeaking}
            disabled={speakingStatus !== "speaking"}
            style={{ fontSize: 13 }}
          >
            Pause
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={resumeSpeaking}
            disabled={speakingStatus !== "paused"}
            style={{ fontSize: 13 }}
          >
            Resume
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={stopSpeaking}
            disabled={speakingStatus === "idle"}
            style={{ fontSize: 13 }}
          >
            Stop
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="reader-zoom-actions" style={{ flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <label htmlFor="purchased-voice">Voice</label>

          <select
            id="purchased-voice"
            value={selectedVoice}
            // onChange={(event) => setSelectedVoice(event.target.value)}
            onChange={(event) => {
              stopSpeaking();
              pagePreparationCacheRef.current.clear();
              setSelectedVoice(event.target.value);
              setSpeakingError("");
            }}
            style={{ maxWidth: 220, padding: 8 }}
          >
            {speechVoices.map((voice) => (
              <option key={voice.voiceURI} value={voice.voiceURI}>
                {voice.name} ({voice.lang})
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <label htmlFor="purchased-speed">Speed</label>

          <select
            id="purchased-speed"
            value={speechRate}
            onChange={(event) => setSpeechRate(Number(event.target.value))}
            style={{ padding: 8 }}
          >
            <option value={0.75}>0.75x</option>
            <option value={0.9}>0.9x</option>
            <option value={1}>1x</option>
            <option value={1.15}>1.15x</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        {speakingError && (
          <p role="alert" style={{ color: "#b42318", width: "100%" }}>
            {speakingError}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      <div className="reader-book-viewport">
        <div
          className="reader-book-zoom"
          // style={{ zoom: zoom / 100 }}
          style={{ zoom: (zoom / 100) * 1.5 }}

        >
          <section className="book-stage">
            <HTMLFlipBook
              key={productId}
              ref={flipBookRef}
              width={380}
              height={520}
              size="stretch"
              minWidth={260}
              maxWidth={760}
              minHeight={360}
              maxHeight={620}
              showCover
              usePortrait
              mobileScrollSupport
              drawShadow
              flippingTime={650}
              maxShadowOpacity={0.45}
              // onFlip={(event) => setCurrentPage(event.data)}

              onFlip={(event) => {
                const nextPage = event.data;
                setCurrentPage(nextPage);

                // if (autoFlipTargetRef.current === nextPage) {
                //   autoFlipTargetRef.current = null;

                //   if (speechTimeoutRef.current) {
                //     clearTimeout(speechTimeoutRef.current);
                //     speechTimeoutRef.current = null;
                //   }

                //   const continuation = autoFlipContinuationRef.current;

                //   if (typeof continuation === "function") {
                //     continuation();
                //   }

                //   return;
                // }

                if (autoFlipTargetRef.current === nextPage) {
                  if (autoFlipTimeoutRef.current) {
                    clearTimeout(autoFlipTimeoutRef.current);
                    autoFlipTimeoutRef.current = null;
                  }

                  const continuation = autoFlipContinuationRef.current;

                  if (typeof continuation === "function") {
                    continuation();
                  }

                  return;
                }

                if (speechStatusRef.current !== "idle") {
                  stopSpeaking();
                }
              }}

              className="book-flip"
            >
              {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => (
                <PurchasedPage
                  key={`${productId}-${index}`}
                  pdfDocument={pdfDocument}
                  number={index + 1}
                  shouldRender={Math.abs(index - currentPage) <= 3}
                  zoom={zoom}
                />
              ))}
            </HTMLFlipBook>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="reader-controls">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={goPrevious}
          disabled={currentPage === 0}
        >
          Previous
        </button>

        <span>
          Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
        </span>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={goNext}
          disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
        >
          Next
        </button>

        <button type="button" onClick={testCurrentPageText} disabled={!docxUrl}>
          Test Speaking Text
        </button>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import mammoth from "mammoth";
import { createWorker } from "tesseract.js";

const docxTextCache = new Map();

// const ocrPageCache = new WeakMap();

const ocrPageCache = new WeakMap();
// let ocrWorkerPromise = null;
const ocrWorkerPromise = new Map();

// function getOCRLanguage(language) {
//   const lang = String(language || "").toLowerCase();

//   if (lang.startsWith("en")) return "eng";
//   if (lang.startsWith("mr")) return "mar";
//   if (lang.startsWith("hi")) return "hin";

//   return "mar+hin";
// }

function getOCRLanguage(language) {
  const lang = String(language || "").toLowerCase();

  if (lang === "mixed") return "eng+hin";

  if (lang.startsWith("en")) return "eng";
  if (lang.startsWith("mr")) return "mar";
  if (lang.startsWith("hi")) return "hin";

  return "mar+hin";
}

// async function getOCRWorker() {
//   if (!ocrWorkerPromise) {
//     ocrWorkerPromise = createWorker(["mar", "hin"]).catch((err) => {
//       ocrWorkerPromise = null;
//       throw err;
//     });
//   }

//   return ocrWorkerPromise;
// }

async function getOCRWorker(language = "mr-IN") {
  const ocrLanguage = getOCRLanguage(language);

  if (!ocrWorkerPromise.has(ocrLanguage)) {
    // const workerPromise = createWorker([ocrLanguage]).catch((error) => {
    // const workerPromise = createWorker(
    //   ocrLanguage === "mar+hin" ? ["mar", "hin"] : [ocrLanguage],
    // ).catch((error) => {
    //   ocrWorkerPromise.delete(ocrLanguage);
    //   throw error;
    // });

    const workerPromise = createWorker(ocrLanguage.split("+")).catch(
      (error) => {
        ocrWorkerPromise.delete(ocrLanguage);
        throw error;
      },
    );

    ocrWorkerPromise.set(ocrLanguage, workerPromise);
    console.log("OCR LANGUAGE SELECTED:", ocrLanguage);
  }

  console.log("OCR LANGUAGE SELECTED:", ocrLanguage);

  return ocrWorkerPromise.get(ocrLanguage);
}

export async function extractDocxText(docxUrl) {
  if (!docxTextCache.has(docxUrl)) {
    const request = (async () => {
      const response = await fetch(docxUrl);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`DOCX loading failed: ${response.status}`);
      }

      const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
      const result = await mammoth.extractRawText({ arrayBuffer });

      return result.value.trim();
    })();

    docxTextCache.set(docxUrl, request);
  }

  try {
    return await docxTextCache.get(docxUrl);
  } catch (error) {
    docxTextCache.delete(docxUrl);
    throw error;
  }
}

const bookLanguageCache = new Map();

export async function detectBookOCRLanguage(docxUrl) {
  if (!docxUrl) {
    return "mr-IN";
  }

  if (!bookLanguageCache.has(docxUrl)) {
    const request = (async () => {
      const text = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);

      const devanagariCount = (text.match(/[\u0900-\u097F]/g) || []).length;

      const englishCount = (text.match(/[A-Za-z]/g) || []).length;

      const detectedLanguage =
        devanagariCount >= englishCount ? "mr-IN" : "en-US";

      console.log("BOOK LANGUAGE DETECTED:", detectedLanguage);
      console.log("DEVANAGARI CHARACTERS:", devanagariCount);
      console.log("ENGLISH CHARACTERS:", englishCount);

      return detectedLanguage;
    })();

    bookLanguageCache.set(docxUrl, request);
  }

  try {
    return await bookLanguageCache.get(docxUrl);
  } catch (error) {
    bookLanguageCache.delete(docxUrl);
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function getBookText({ docxUrl, pdfDocument, pageNumber }) {
  if (docxUrl) {
    const text = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);

    return {
      source: "docx",
      text,
      pageAligned: false,
    };
  }

  if (!pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("PDF document and page number are required");
  }

  const page = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
  const content = await page.getTextContent();

  return {
    source: "pdf",
    text: content.items
      .map((item) => item.str || "")
      .join(" ")
      .trim(),
    pageAligned: true,
  };
}

export async function getReadablePageText({
  docxUrl,
  pdfDocument,
  pageNumber,
  previewTextUrl,
  // language = "mr-IN",
  language,
}) {
  if (!pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("PDF document and page number are required.");
  }

  // Admin and purchased reader: original matching logic.
  if (docxUrl) {
    // const detectedLanguage = await detectBookOCRLanguage(docxUrl);

    const detectedLanguage = language === "mixed"
    ? "mixed"
    : await detectBookOCRLanguage(docxUrl);

    const result = await extractVerifiedDocxPage({
      docxUrl,
      pdfDocument,
      pageNumber,
      language: detectedLanguage,
    });

    if (!result.text?.trim()) {
      throw new Error("No readable text found on this page.");
    }

    return result.text;
  }

  // Public preview: only first 8 pages.
  if (previewTextUrl) {
    if (pageNumber > 8) {
      throw new Error("Only 8 preview pages are available.");
    }

    // const ocrText = await testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber);
    const ocrText = await testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber, language);

    const response = await fetch(previewTextUrl, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        pageNumber,
        ocrText,
      }),
    });

    const data = await response.json();

    if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
      throw new Error(data.message || "Unable to prepare preview speech.");
    }

    return data.data.text;
  }

  throw new Error("Clean DOCX text is unavailable for this book.");
}

export async function testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber, language = "mr-IN") {
  if (
    !pdfDocument ||
    !Number.isInteger(pageNumber) ||
    pageNumber < 1 ||
    pageNumber > pdfDocument.numPages
  ) {
    throw new Error("Valid PDF document and page number are required.");
  }
  // Diagnostic only: check whether PDF has a usable text layer.
  // Do not bypass OCR yet.
  try {
    const pdfPage = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);

    console.time(`PDF TEXT LAYER ${pageNumber}`);
    const textContent = await pdfPage.getTextContent();

    const pdfText = textContent.items
      .map((item) => item.str || "")
      .join(" ")
      .trim();

    console.timeEnd(`PDF TEXT LAYER ${pageNumber}`);

    console.log("PDF TEXT LENGTH:", pdfText.length);
    console.log("PDF TEXT SAMPLE:", pdfText.slice(0, 300));
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("PDF text layer check failed:", error);
  }

  let pageCache = ocrPageCache.get(pdfDocument);

  if (!pageCache) {
    pageCache = new Map();
    ocrPageCache.set(pdfDocument, pageCache);
  }

  const cacheKey = `${pageNumber}:${getOCRLanguage(language)}`;
  // if (!pageCache.has(pageNumber)) {
  if (!pageCache.has(cacheKey)) {
    const request = (async () => {
      const page = await pdfDocument.getPage(pageNumber);
      // const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 2 });
      const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });

      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

      canvas.width = Math.ceil(viewport.width);
      canvas.height = Math.ceil(viewport.height);

      try {
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

        if (!context) {
          throw new Error("Canvas context is unavailable.");
        }

        await page.render({
          canvasContext: context,
          viewport,
        }).promise;

        // const worker = await getOCRWorker();
        const worker = await getOCRWorker(language);
        const result = await worker.recognize(canvas);

        return {
          text: result.data.text.trim(),
          confidence: result.data.confidence,
        };
      } finally {
        canvas.width = 0;
        canvas.height = 0;
      }
    })();

    // pageCache.set(pageNumber, request);
    pageCache.set(cacheKey, request);
  }

  try {
    // const result = await pageCache.get(pageNumber);
    const result = await pageCache.get(cacheKey);

    console.log("OCR PAGE:", pageNumber);
    console.log("OCR CONFIDENCE:", result.confidence);
    console.log("OCR TEXT:", result.text);

    return result.text;
  } catch (error) {
    // pageCache.delete(pageNumber);
    pageCache.delete(cacheKey);
    throw error;
  }
}

function normalizeForMatching(text) {
  return (
    text
      .normalize("NFC")
      .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, "")
      // .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]/gu, "")
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{M}\p{N}]/gu, "")
      .toLowerCase()
  );
}

function getTextParagraphs(text) {
  return text
    .split(/\n+/)
    .map((paragraph) => paragraph.trim())
    .filter((paragraph) => paragraph.length > 20);
}

export async function testDocxPageMatch({
  docxUrl,
  pdfDocument,
  pageNumber,
  language = "mr-IN",
}) {
  if (!docxUrl || !pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("DOCX URL, PDF document and page number are required.");
  }

  const startTime = performance.now();

  console.time(`OCR PAGE ${pageNumber}`);
  // const ocrText = await testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber);
  const ocrText = await testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber, language);
  // const ocrText = await testPageOCR(pdfDocument, pageNumber, language, "mr-IN");
  console.timeEnd(`OCR PAGE ${pageNumber}`);

  console.time(`DOCX PAGE ${pageNumber}`);
  const docxText = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);
  console.timeEnd(`DOCX PAGE ${pageNumber}`);

  console.log(
    `TOTAL PREPARATION PAGE ${pageNumber}:`,
    ((performance.now() - startTime) / 1000).toFixed(2),
    "seconds",
  );

  const ocrLines = ocrText
    .split("\n")
    .map((line) => line.trim())
    .filter((line) => normalizeForMatching(line).length > 25);

  const paragraphs = getTextParagraphs(docxText);

  const matches = [];

  for (const ocrLine of ocrLines) {
    const normalizedOCR = normalizeForMatching(ocrLine);

    for (let i = 0; i < paragraphs.length; i++) {
      const normalizedDOCX = normalizeForMatching(paragraphs[i]);

      if (normalizedDOCX.includes(normalizedOCR)) {
        matches.push({
          ocrLine,
          paragraphIndex: i,
          docxParagraph: paragraphs[i],
        });

        break;
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("PAGE MATCH TEST:", pageNumber);
  console.log("OCR LINES:", ocrLines.length);
  console.log("EXACT MATCHES:", matches.length);
  console.log("MATCH DETAILS:", matches);

  return {
    pageNumber,
    ocrLines: ocrLines.length,
    exactMatches: matches.length,
    matches,
  };
}

export async function testCleanPageBoundaries({
  docxUrl,
  pdfDocument,
  pageNumber,
}) {
  const result = await testDocxPageMatch({
    docxUrl,
    pdfDocument,
    pageNumber,
  });

  const indexes = [
    ...new Set(result.matches.map((match) => match.paragraphIndex)),
  ].sort((a, b) => a - b);

  if (result.exactMatches < 5 || indexes.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("Not enough matches to identify this page.");
  }

  const docxText = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);
  const paragraphs = getTextParagraphs(docxText);

  const firstIndex = indexes[0];
  const lastIndex = indexes[indexes.length - 1];

  console.log("PAGE:", pageNumber);
  console.log("FIRST PARAGRAPH:", firstIndex);
  console.log("LAST PARAGRAPH:", lastIndex);

  console.log("FIRST PARAGRAPH TEXT:", paragraphs[firstIndex]);
  console.log("LAST PARAGRAPH TEXT:", paragraphs[lastIndex]);

  return {
    firstIndex,
    lastIndex,
    firstParagraph: paragraphs[firstIndex],
    lastParagraph: paragraphs[lastIndex],
  };
}

function normalizeWithPositions(text) {
  const characters = [];
  const positions = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const originalCharacter = text[i];

    const normalized = originalCharacter
      .normalize("NFC")
      .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, "")
      // .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]/gu, "")
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{M}\p{N}]/gu, "")
      .toLowerCase();

    for (const character of normalized) {
      characters.push(character);
      positions.push(i);
    }
  }

  return {
    normalized: characters.join(""),
    positions,
  };
}

const cleanPageTextCache = new WeakMap();

// export async function extractVerifiedDocxPage({
//   docxUrl,
//   pdfDocument,
//   pageNumber,
// }) {
export async function extractVerifiedDocxPage({
  docxUrl,
  pdfDocument,
  pageNumber,
  language,
}) {
  if (!docxUrl || !pdfDocument || !pageNumber) {
    throw new Error("PDF, DOCX and page number are required.");
  }

  let documentCache = cleanPageTextCache.get(pdfDocument);

  if (!documentCache) {
    documentCache = new Map();
    cleanPageTextCache.set(pdfDocument, documentCache);
  }

  // const cacheKey = `${docxUrl}:${pageNumber}`;
  const cacheKey = `${docxUrl}:${pageNumber}:${getOCRLanguage(language)}`;
  if (!documentCache.has(cacheKey)) {
    const request = (async () => {
      const matchResult = await testDocxPageMatch({
        docxUrl,
        pdfDocument,
        pageNumber,
        language,
      });

      const { matches, ocrLines, exactMatches } = matchResult;

      // if (
      //   exactMatches < 10 ||
      //   exactMatches / ocrLines < 0.7
      // ) {
      //   throw new Error(
      //     "Page matching confidence is too low. Reading stopped."
      //   );
      // }

      const matchRatio = ocrLines > 0 ? exactMatches / ocrLines : 0;

      // const requiredMatches = Math.min(
      //   10,
      //   Math.max(5, Math.ceil(ocrLines * 0.7)),
      // );

      const requiredMatches = Math.min(
        10,
        Math.max(2, Math.ceil(ocrLines * 0.7)),
      );

      // if (exactMatches < requiredMatches || matchRatio < 0.7) {
      //   throw new Error(
      //     "Page matching confidence is too low. Reading stopped.",
      //   );
      // }

      if (exactMatches < requiredMatches || matchRatio < 0.5) {
        throw new Error(
          "Page matching confidence is too low. Reading stopped.",
        );
      }

      const docxText = await extractDocxText(docxUrl);
      const paragraphs = getTextParagraphs(docxText);

      const indexes = matches.map((match) => match.paragraphIndex);

      // Matches should progress forward through the DOCX.
      for (let i = 1; i < indexes.length; i++) {
        if (indexes[i] < indexes[i - 1]) {
          throw new Error("DOCX matches are out of order. Reading stopped.");
        }
      }

      const firstMatch = matches[0];
      const lastMatch = matches[matches.length - 1];

      const firstIndex = firstMatch.paragraphIndex;
      const lastIndex = lastMatch.paragraphIndex;

      const selectedParagraphs = paragraphs.slice(firstIndex, lastIndex + 1);

      const combinedText = selectedParagraphs.join("\n\n");
      const mapped = normalizeWithPositions(combinedText);

      const normalizedStart = normalizeForMatching(firstMatch.ocrLine);

      const normalizedEnd = normalizeForMatching(lastMatch.ocrLine);

      const startPosition = mapped.normalized.indexOf(normalizedStart);

      const endPosition = mapped.normalized.lastIndexOf(normalizedEnd);

      if (
        startPosition === -1 ||
        endPosition === -1 ||
        endPosition < startPosition
      ) {
        throw new Error("Unable to identify clean page boundaries.");
      }

      const originalStart = mapped.positions[startPosition];

      const endCharacterIndex = endPosition + normalizedEnd.length - 1;

      const originalEnd = mapped.positions[endCharacterIndex] + 1;

      const cleanText = combinedText.slice(originalStart, originalEnd).trim();

      if (!cleanText) {
        throw new Error("Clean page text is empty.");
      }

      return {
        pageNumber,
        text: cleanText,
        exactMatches,
        ocrLines,
        firstIndex,
        lastIndex,
      };
    })();

    documentCache.set(cacheKey, request);
  }

  try {
    return await documentCache.get(cacheKey);
  } catch (err) {
    documentCache.delete(cacheKey);
    throw err;
  }
}
